- 1 - 
海外產業訊息評論 
全球生成式 AI 模型開發商 Anthropic(ANT US)上市前瞻 
企業需求帶動營收高成長，算力成本考驗高估值

報告日期：2026.11.9 研究員：吳俞潔

10 月 26 日 Anthropic(ANT US) 向美國 SEC 提交 IPO 申請，11 月 2 日更
新第三季財報，市場預期公司將於 11 月底前上市，估值達 1.5 至 2 兆。
Anthropic 旗下 Claude 主要應用於企業 AI、 軟體開發及Agent 工作流程 ，
受惠 Claude Code 及企業 API 需求快速成長，26Q3 營收達 169.6 億(季增
47%)，2026 年前三季累計營收 332 億。目前普遍預估公司 2026 年營收有
望達 580 億 ， 若以Palantir 做為高成長的企業 AI 軟體作為基準，Anthropic
也受惠企業 AI 支出增加， 營收增速高於Palantir，但未來大量算力成本以及
現金流穩定度不如成熟軟體公司， 對應27 至 30 倍 Forward P/S，Anthropic 
IPO 估值達 1.6 至 1.8 兆，引發讓市場對企業 AI 商業化及模型公司估值的想
像空間。 
不過，兆豐認為 Anthropic 雖然已經證明企業願意為 AI 模型付費，但目前
1.6 至 1.8 兆估值已經提前反應未來數年營收維持高速成長，以及算力成本
持續改善的預期。公司 26Q3 營收季增 47%，但因算力及基礎設施費用季增
77%，導致營益率由上季 19%降至 15%，顯示收入增加並未帶來相同比例的
獲利成長。另外，公司已經簽訂 8,954 億的長期算力承諾，未來仍然需要擴
大 Token 需求才能支撐相關支出，考量企業 AI 需求持續成長，但模型價格
競爭及算力成本有壓縮利潤率風險， 因此兆豐目前對Anthropic 的 IPO 估值
持中性看待。

Claude Code 帶動企業 AI 需求，Anthropic 收入主要來自 Token 用量 
Anthropic 成立於 2021 年，由前 OpenAI 研究人員 Dario Amodei 創立，主
要開發 Claude 系列 AI 模型，透過 Claude 訂閱服務、企業 API 及 Claude 
Code 產品取得收入。相比傳統 SaaS 以固定訂閱費用為主，Anthropic 80%
營收來自 Token 使用量計價 ， 訂閱收入占20%， 企業可以透過AWS、Google 
Cloud 的雲端平台採購 Claude 服務，CSP 客戶占公司收入 48%，這種模式
使得 Anthropic 能隨著企業 AI 使用量增加而擴大收入，但也導致營收受到
Token 價格及企業使用量變動影響。 
受惠 Claude Code 及企業 API 需求成長，過去 12 個月支出超過 10 萬的客
戶達 11,900 家，其中超過 1,900 家年支出超過 100 萬，250 家超過 1,000

### Page 2

評等分級：買進 （預期未來六個月股價漲幅超過+30%）; 逢低買進 （預期未來六個月股價漲幅介於+15%與+30%之間）; 區間操作
（預期未來六個月股價漲跌幅介於-15%與+15%之間）; 逢高賣出 （預期未來六個月股價跌幅介於-15%與-30%之間） ；賣出 （預期
未來六個月股價跌幅超過-30%）。 
本刊所刊載之內容僅做為參考，惟已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量
投資之需求與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任。任何轉載或引用本報告內容必須經本公司同意。  
106.4.14 版 
- 2 - 
海外產業訊息評論 
萬，企業 AI 支出正轉向更大規模的付費使用，也帶動 Anthropic 營收快速成
長。

估值達 1.8 兆，Token 價格決定高成長能否持續 
Anthropic 2026 年前三季營收達 332 億(年增 12 倍)，26Q3 營收 170 億(季
增 47%)，但相比 Q2 季增 144%成長大幅放緩。目前普遍預估公司 2026 年
營收達 580 億，若以 Palantir 作為高成長企業 AI 軟體的估值參考 ，合理倍數
落在 27 至 30 倍 Forward P/S，對應 IPO 估值 1.6 至 1.8 兆，兆豐認為雖然
Anthropic 營收成長的確遠高於多數軟體公司，Claude Code 及企業 API 的
高需求也支持未來營收持續擴張，但但模型訓練及推論需要持續投入大量算
力 ， 獲利率及現金流穩定度不如成熟軟體公司，以 2026 年 580 億營收估算，
隱含 28 至 31 倍 Forward P/S，反應了市場不只預期公司維持高成長，也預
期未來利潤率能隨著規模擴大而改善。 
Anthropic 80%收入來自 Token 用量計價 ， 未來營收不只取決於企業使用量 ，
也包含模型能否維持目前的高階定價， 外資8 月調查 100 名歐美大型企業 AI
預算決策者，92%預期未來 12 個月 Token 預算增加，但若 Token 價格上漲
50%，84%企業會優化使用或轉換其他模型，若價格下降 50%，86%企業則
願意增加使用量，反應企業 AI 需求仍有成長空間，但客戶對價格相當敏感，
尤其開放權重模型及低成本模型持續改善，企業有機會將部分工作轉向更為
便宜的模型，即使整體 Token 使用量增加，但開放權重和低成本模型競爭，
Anthropic 面臨單價競爭導致營收增速放緩的風險，因此兆豐認為目前 1.6 至
1.8 兆估值已經反應 Anthropic 長期維持高成長及較高 Token 價格的預期，
但如果模型競爭加劇，導致價格下降速度超過增加的使用量，將有估值下修
風險。

接近 9,000 億的算力承諾，收入成長未必同步轉為獲利 
Anthropic 為了支撐 Claude 模型訓練及企業推論需求 ， 持續擴大與Amazon、
Google、Microsoft 及其他 AI 基礎設施供應商的合作 ， 並且透過Nvidia GPU、
Google TPU 及 AWS Trainium 分散算力來源，Anthropic 目前即時可用算力
達 2.7GW，與 OpenAI 的 3.2GW 差距縮小，近期再透過 Nscale、Lambda
及 SpaceX 取得 1.1GW 的新增算力協議。不過，算力擴張也帶來龐大的長
期採購支出 ，Anthropic 已經簽訂 8,954 億的長期算力承諾 ， 其中4,475 億預
計在 2030 年底前兌現， 未來12 個月到期的承諾達 698 億，合約有最低採購
要求，即使未來需求不如預期，公司仍然需要負擔相關成本。 
從財務表現來看，Anthropic 26Q3 營收季增 47%，但算力及基礎設施費用季
增 77%，營益率也由 19%下降至 15%，顯示新增收入並未帶來相同比例的
獲利成長，雖然公司提到部分成本增加來自短期高價算力採購，未來隨著長
期合約及專用資料中心陸續上線，單位算力成本有機會下降，但兆豐認為即
使採購成本改善，也不代表利潤率一定回升，主要因 Anthropic 提前承諾大
規模算力支出，但未來 Token 收入仍然取決於企業實際使用量及模型定價，

### Page 3

評等分級：買進 （預期未來六個月股價漲幅超過+30%）; 逢低買進 （預期未來六個月股價漲幅介於+15%與+30%之間）; 區間操作
（預期未來六個月股價漲跌幅介於-15%與+15%之間）; 逢高賣出 （預期未來六個月股價跌幅介於-15%與-30%之間） ；賣出 （預期
未來六個月股價跌幅超過-30%）。 
本刊所刊載之內容僅做為參考，惟已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量
投資之需求與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任。任何轉載或引用本報告內容必須經本公司同意。  
106.4.14 版 
- 3 - 
海外產業訊息評論 
若新增算力無法帶來足夠的 Token 收入 ， 固定採購成本將壓縮獲利及現金流。 
因此兆豐認為 Anthropic 目前估值不只反應營收持續高成長，也隱含未來算
力成本下降能帶動利潤率改善，但考量 26Q3 營收增速大幅放緩，營益率下
滑、未來長期算力採購承諾龐大，以及 Token 需求成長無法抵銷模型降價及
新增算力成本 ， 將壓縮利潤率 ， 不足以支撐更高的高成長成熟企業 AI 軟體公
司估值，因此兆豐對 1.6 至 1.8 兆的 IPO 估值持中性看待

（本資料僅供內部參考，不作為投資決策依據）

國際經濟金融週報內容摘要 
美 
國 
內需與房市提供支撐 美國財政及通膨風險升高 
經濟 
⚫ 9 月美國零售與餐飲服務銷售額為 7,450 億美元 ， 月增0.8%、 年增6.3%；
扣除汽車及零件銷售後月增 0.9%，進一步扣除汽車、零件及加油站後
之核心零售銷售月增 0.2%。加油站、餐飲及非實體零售銷售持續成長，
顯示美國消費需求仍具韌性，惟成長動能較前月略有放緩。 
⚫ 2026 財年美國聯邦政府財政赤字達 1 兆 9,924.7 億美元，年增 12.2%，
主因政府支出成長高於收入。9 月單月由去年同期盈餘轉為 269 億美元
赤字，關稅退稅增加壓縮收入，而全年淨利息支出突破1 兆美元、年增
8.4%。在聯邦債務總額逾 40 兆美元下，財政赤字擴大及債務融資成本
攀升，持續考驗財政永續性。 
⚫ 9 月美國成屋銷售年率 399 萬戶，月增 1.5%、年減 2.2%；成屋中位數
售價 41.75 萬美元，年增 1.3%，連續 39 個月上漲。待售庫存增至 160
萬戶 、 供給量4.8 個月 ， 顯示供給改善 ， 惟高利率環境仍抑制購屋需求 。 
⚫ 9 月美國消費者物價指數 (CPI)經季節調整後月增 0.6%，高於 8 月之
0.4%，年增率由 3.4%升至 3.7%；核心 CPI 月增 0.3%，年增率由 2.4%
升至 2.6%。受能源價格上揚影響，能源指數月增 5.2%，其中汽油價格
大幅上漲，成為推升通膨之主要因素；住房指數續增0.4%。整體而言，
美國通膨壓力較前月升溫，核心物價漲勢亦略有增強。 
⚫ 9 月美國生產者物價指數(PPI)月增 1.3%，年增率由 5.6%升至 6.3%；能
源價格大漲 10.8%為主要推升因素 ， 帶動商品價格月增2.6%。 服務價格
亦月增 0.8%，核心 PPI 年增率由 4.8%升至 5.0%，顯示生產端通膨壓力
持續升高。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 10 月中旬，多位聯準會官員表示通膨仍具壓力。Mary Daly 認為未來可
能仍需升息，但 10 月底會議無迫切行動必要；Beth Hammack 警告若通
膨預期惡化，可能加大緊縮力道；主席 Kevin Warsh 則指出美國經濟仍
具韌性，但能源價格上漲及 AI 投資擴張恐推升通膨壓力。整體而言，
聯準會立場偏鷹，但短期升息時點仍視通膨走勢而定。 
⚫ 美墨關稅談判原接近達成初步協議 ， 惟美國商務部長反對調降汽車及鋼
鋁關稅，使協商進展受阻。墨西哥則爭取降低汽車關稅及擴大零組件抵

中 
國 
AI 相關需求帶動出口動能強勁成長 
經濟 
⚫ 9 月出口年增率較 8 月大幅增加 11.3 個百分點至 36.3%，出口總值為
4,472 億美元 。 進口年增率增2.8 個百分點至 31%， 進口總值為3,137 億
美元。 
⚫ 9 月生產者通膨(出廠價格)由 8 月的 3.8%升至 5%。 
⚫ 9 月消費者通膨(CPI)升 0.1 個百分點為 0.9%。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 歐中貿易談判公布相關細節 ， 未來中國出口至歐盟的混合動力汽車設定
每年 35 萬輛的配額上限(每年可遞增 6%)。依照歐盟推算，中國對歐盟
的混合動力車出口量將較原先潛在規模減少一半以上。 
短評 
⚫ 雖歐中貿易協議不利於汽車出口 ， 但有助於避免歐盟進一步擴大貿易保

一、 美國—內需與房市提供支撐 美國財政及通膨風險升高 
2026 年 9 月美國零售與餐飲服務銷售額經季節性調整後為 7,450 億美元，較
上月增加 0.8%，年增 6.3%。若扣除汽車及零件銷售，零售額月增 0.9%；進一步
扣除汽車、零件及加油站後，核心零售銷售月增0.2%，顯示整體消費需求仍維持
成長。從主要業別表現觀察，加油站、非實體零售商及餐飲服務與飲酒場所銷售
額均較上月增加，其中加油站及餐飲消費表現相對亮眼，電子及家電商店銷售亦
呈成長。整體而言，9 月美國零售銷售延續擴張走勢，顯示民間消費仍具支撐力
道，且多數主要消費類別維持正成長，惟核心零售增幅較前月放緩，後續仍須關
注物價、就業及金融情勢變化對消費支出的影響。 
2026 年 9 月(2026 財年第 12 個月)美國聯邦政府財政赤字為 269.0 億美元 ， 較
去年同期 1,979.4 億美元財政盈餘轉為赤字。當月政府收入 5,578.8 億美元，年增
2.6%；政府支出 5,847.8 億美元，年增 69.1%，支出增幅明顯高於收入。收入方
面，9 月關稅淨收入由 8 月的 128.4 億美元降至 59.1 億美元，累計2026 財年關稅
淨收入約 1,732.2 億美元，年減11.1%，關稅退稅增加已部分抵銷關稅收入成長。
支出方面，9 月聯邦政府淨利息支出約 347.9 億美元，全年累計達1 兆 517.5 億美
元 ， 年增8.4%， 反映高利率環境下債務利息負擔持續攀升 。 累計2026 財年全年 ，
美國聯邦政府財政赤字約 1 兆 9,924.7 億美元，較 2025 財年擴大 12.2%。其中，
政府收入年增 3.2%至 5 兆 4,033.3 億美元 ， 支出年增5.5%至 7 兆 3,958.0 億美元 ，
顯示支出成長仍快於收入增加。從支出結構觀察，社會安全、醫療保險、國防及
淨利息支出均較上年增加，持續推升財政壓力。整體而言，美國財政赤字擴大主
要反映政府支出成長強勁、利息負擔居高不下，以及關稅退稅增加削弱部分收入
效果 。 在聯邦債務總額突破40 兆美元背景下 ， 未來財政赤字走勢 、 債務融資成本
及財政永續性問題，仍將是市場關注的重要焦點。 
2026 年 9 月美國成屋銷售經季節調整後年率升至 399 萬戶 ， 較上月增加1.5%，
惟較去年同期減少 2.2%，整體房市成交動能仍偏疲弱。區域表現方面，南部及西
部分別月增 2.8%，中西部持平，東北部月減 2.1%，四大地區銷售量均較去年同
期下滑。房價方面，成屋售價中位數為 41.75 萬美元，年增 1.3%，已連續 39 個

機票、休閒娛樂及服飾等價格上漲，通訊價格則下跌。整體而言，9 月美國通膨
明顯回升，主要受汽油價格大幅上漲及住房成本續增帶動；核心 CPI 月增率維持
0.3%，年增率則回升至 2.6%，顯示核心物價壓力較前月略有增強，未來通膨走勢
仍須持續關注能源價格變化及住房等服務項目價格表現。 
 2026 年 9 月美國生產者物價指數(PPI)經季節調整後月增 1.3%，高於上月之
0.5%，年增率則由 5.6%升至 6.3%。其中，最終需求商品價格月增 2.6%，主要受
能源價格月增 10.8%帶動，以汽油、柴油及航空燃油價格漲幅較為顯著；食品價
格亦月增 1.2%。另一方面，最終需求服務價格月增 0.8%，主要反映批發及零售
利潤率、運輸服務價格上升。剔除食品、能源及貿易服務後之核心最終需求 PPI
月增 0.5%，高於上月之 0.3%，年增率由 4.8%升至 5.0%。整體而言，9 月 PPI 升
幅明顯擴大，主要反映能源與服務價格同步上揚，顯示生產端成本壓力仍偏高，
後續仍須關注能源價格及運輸成本變化對通膨走勢之影響。

先觀察後續數據表現。克里夫蘭聯準銀行總裁哈瑪克(Beth Hammack)則警告，若
通膨預期持續惡化，聯準會可能必須採取更積極的緊縮措施，以維持物價穩定。

近期美墨貿易談判再度受到關稅爭議影響。據《華爾街日報》報導，雙方原

Lutnick 反對大幅調降目前對墨西哥汽車課徵的 25%關稅 ， 以及鋼鐵 、 鋁製品與部
分衍生製成品的 50%關稅，使談判進展受阻。墨西哥方面則希望將汽車關稅降至
15%或以下，以縮小與日本、南韓汽車適用稅率的差距，並擴大汽車關稅抵減機
制對美墨加三國零組件價值的認列範圍；惟美方仍須考量國內金屬產業對降稅可
能衝擊本土生產及就業的疑慮。儘管雙方對關稅調整幅度仍存分歧，墨西哥經貿
官員表示協商已有部分進展，美國商務部亦強調持續與墨方保持溝通，顯示談判
尚未正式破局。整體而言，美墨關稅協商結果不僅攸關北美汽車及金屬產業供應
鏈成本與競爭力，也可能影響 USMCA 後續檢討與延長協商進程；若雙方遲遲無
法縮小分歧，恐增加企業投資與供應鏈布局的不確定性，並對北美區域貿易整合
形成壓力。 
二、歐元區—工業生產走疲，前景有待觀察 
 歐元區 8 月工業生產(不含營造業)較 7 月下滑 0.5%，呈現連續三個月下降，
其中非耐久消費財、中間財及能源產出分別減少 2.7%、0.8%、0.6%，僅資本財、
耐久消費財產出分別成長 0.4%、0.2%。儘管德國 8 月工業生產(不含營造業)反彈
0.4%，係受惠車廠陸續結束歲修，但法、義、西工業生產分別下跌0.3%、1.3%、
0.8%為主要拖累因素，反映先前美伊戰爭帶來的庫存儲備需求逐漸消退，尤其以
能源密集產業(化工、造紙、 鋼鐵等)變化最為明顯。整體而言，即便排除能源密集
產業的波動，其他製造業諸如製藥、汽車、機械等生產情形皆偏疲軟。

影響企業以及政府在各項支出 、 投資決策 ， 進而形成拖累因素 。 另外 ， 歐盟的 「下

三、日本—AI 投資續強，帶動機械訂單穩健復甦 
 8 月核心機械訂單(扣除波動訂單)月增 5.2%，其中製造業訂單月增 1.6%，人

四、中國—AI 相關需求帶動出口動能強勁成長 
9 月出口年增率較 8 月大幅增加 11.3 個百分點至 36.3%，出口總值為 4,472
億美元 ； 累計前九個月出口年增率上升為21.3%， 累積出口總值為33,707 億美元 。
前九個月出口主要成長項目(累積年增率)包含 IC(108.1%)、自動數據處理設備(包
含伺服器等，54.6%)、船舶(29.9%)、汽車(54.3%)，其中 IC 及自動數據處理設備
出口加速成長，為 9 月出口年增率大幅上升的主要因素。 
9 月進口年增率較 8 月增 2.8 個百分點至 31%，進口總值為 3,137 億美元；累
計前九個月進口值成長 27.5%至 24,326 億美元。其中 IC、自動數據處理設備(包
含伺服器等)進口分別成長 63.5%、124.8%，呼應中國對高階 IC 的依賴，以及部
分伺服器關鍵組件有賴於進口滿足所需。進出口相抵後，前九個月貿易順差達
9,381 億美元，僅較去年同期增加 7.7%，增幅明顯低於出口，顯示出口擴張同時
伴隨關鍵原物料進口大幅增加，稀釋出口成長的加值效果，亦反映部分高附加價
值產品依賴國際供應鏈支持。 
9 月生產者通膨(出廠價格)由 8 月的 3.8%升至 5%， 主因原油價格自7 月起走
升，其影響逐漸反映於上游生產成本。當月石油和天然氣開採業出廠價格年增率
續揚 14.1 個百分點至 24.5%，並帶動燃料加工業出廠價格年增率上揚至 21.6%，
此外，對於原油價格波動較為敏感的化學品、化纖出廠價格年增率加速升至
12.1%、12.3%，顯示原油價格上漲的影響開始擴散至中下游產業。此外，由於近
期鋼鐵價格走弱 ， 黑色金屬礦產開採 、 冶煉加工業出廠價格分別年減3.4%、0.2%，

9 月消費者通膨(CPI)升 0.1 個百分點為 0.9%，但仍然是今年以來第三低的水
準，核心通膨(不含食品及能源)持平於 1 %不變。該月燃油通膨續增 6.6 個百分點
至 14.9%，在客運成本增加之下，旅遊服務通膨亦增加 0.8 個百分點至 3.5%，此
外，由於手機價格上漲，通信工具通膨上揚 3.7 個百分點至 14.3%；其他商品及
服務價格則大致平穩。整體而言，雖然企業生產成本持續上升，但在民間消費乏
力之下，企業仍大多自行吸收成本，加上政府壓抑零售燃油及民生水電，故目前
原油價格高漲的影響尚未反映於消費端價格。惟考量能源成本傳導存在時間差，
若油價維持高檔或進一步上漲，未來通膨仍有進一步上升的風險。 
展望未來，歐中談判已就汽車貿易達成部分共識，包含未來中國出口至歐盟
的混合動力汽車設定每年 35 萬輛的配額上限，且配額規模可逐年增加 6%。依照
歐盟預估 2026 年自中國進口約 80 萬輛混合動力汽車推算，中國對歐盟的混合動
力車出口量將較原先潛在規模減少一半以上。雖該協議不利於汽車出口，但有助
於避免歐盟進一步擴大貿易保護措施，中國車廠則可能會加速透過赴歐投資設
廠、提升當地生產比重等方式因應配額限制，藉此維持在歐洲市場的競爭力與市
占率。 
五、印度及東南亞—IMF 上調印度及東協的經濟展望；新、馬第三季 GDP 均逾
5% 
(1)據 IMF2026 年 10 月經濟展望報告(WEO)(詳下表)，印度仍是全球主要經濟體
中成長最快者之一，雖然面對油價上漲及全球不確定性，但內需及民間消費韌性
強勁，服務業持續擴張，2026、2027 年 GDP 成長率預估皆為 7.0%。東協國家方
面，受惠於 AI 伺服器與資料中心投資、科技產業供應鏈重組、美中貿易重組帶
來轉單效應等利多，越南、馬來西亞、新加坡、菲律賓及泰國均為 AI 供應鏈受益
國，但須留意地緣政治、全球貿易重組及 AI 投資循環變化，帶來的通膨及經濟
下行風險，建議各國政府提升農業生產效率及糧食安全，提升數位經濟及服務業
競爭力，強化財政紀律並提高公共投資效率，維持穩健貨幣政策與匯率穩定。

(3)新加坡：2026 年第三季經濟成長(GDP)季增 1.1%、年增 5.4%，雖較上季增速
放緩，仍為近年較高的成長增速。外需仍為主要成長引擎，全球 AI 相關設備需
求持續擴張，帶動電子產品與機械設備出口成長，加上航空與航運活動活絡，使
貨物及服務出口表現亮眼；相較之下，內需成長較為溫和，零售消費及部分民生
服務需求仍相對疲弱。從產業面分析，製造業年增7.5%，雖較前季放緩，仍是經
濟成長的重要支柱；營建業受公共及民間工程推進成長加速 8.8%；服務業成長
5.2%，以批發貿易受電子出口帶動表現尤佳。IMF 預期新加坡將持續受惠於 AI、
生技及金融服務發展，預估 2026 年經濟成長達 5.0%、2027 年放緩至 3.0%。 
新加坡金融管理局 10 月政策評估維持新元名目有效匯率政策區間不變，反映 4
月和 7 月提前收緊貨幣政策後，核心通膨符合預期，並預計 2027 年年中隨著供
應驅動型進口通膨放緩和國內 生產成本維持低位 ，核心通膨將明顯回落 ，預計
2026 年核心通膨率平均為 1.5%至 2.0%，2027 年則為 1.5%至 2.5%。MAS 表示目
前的升值政策將使新元名目有效匯率持續走強，並抑制近期輸入型通膨的上升，

(4)馬來西亞：2026 年第三季經濟成長年增率達 5.3%，其中，民間消費受就業市
場穩定、服務業持續擴張及家庭支出成長帶動而維持韌性；投資則受企業擴產及
資料中心等大型專案推進支撐；此外，全球 AI 及科技產業需求強勁，帶動電子
電機產品出口表現，成為外需的重要動能。從產業面分析，製造業成長 加速至
7.5%，為主要成長引擎，係受惠電子、電機及石化相關產業出口增加；服務業、
營建業成長略放慢至 5.4%、6.0%；農業因棕櫚油和畜牧業產量增加從萎縮 3.7%
反彈回升 1.3%， 礦業及採石業因原油與凝析油產量下降而衰退4.9%。 整體而言 ，
馬來西亞經濟呈現「內需穩健、科技出口強勁、投資活絡」的成長格局，IMF 最
新預估 2026 年經濟成長達 5.2%、2027 年放緩至 4.6%。 
六、台灣—AI 帶動出口、投資與消費成長，中經院上修 2026 年經濟預測 
中經院 10 月 16 日公布最新預測，上修 2026 年台灣經濟成長率至 12.16%，
創 1988 年以來新高，主要受惠於 AI 技術快速發展帶動全球科技投資，半導體與
資通訊產品需求強勁，出口維持高速成長，同時民間投資與消費亦同步擴張，經
濟成長動能由內、外需共同支撐。另一方面，由於中東地緣政治風險升溫帶動油
價維持高檔，能源價格外溢效果推升整體物價，中經院將 2026 年 CPI 年增率預
估上修至 2.06%，惟整體通膨仍屬相對溫和。

Indicators Update 
2026年 9月鉱工業生産 
自動車工業が挽回生産で持ち直し、生産全体は3カ月ぶりに上昇

経済調査部 エコノミスト ビリング 安奈

[要約] 
◼ 2026年 9月の生産指数は前月比＋2.2％と、コンセンサス（同＋1.2％、Bloomberg調
査）を上回り、3 カ月ぶりに上昇した。自動車工業は挽回生産で持ち直したほか、AI
関連需要で生産用機械工業なども増産し、生産全体を押し上げた。経済産業省は基調
判断を「一進一退」に据え置いた。 
◼ 先行きの生産指数は緩やかに上昇する見込み。AI関連需要などが引き続き国内生産を
押し上げよう。ただし、金利高による海外需要の下振れなどが生産を下押しするリス
クには注意が必要だ。 
◼ 2026年 11月 9日に公表予定の 9月分の景気動向指数は、先行 CIが前月差▲0.9ptの
117.1、一致CIが同＋0.7ptの 119.5と予想する。この予測値に基づくと、9月の基調
判断は機械的に「改善」に据え置かれる。

### Page 2

1

2 / 7

【生産】自動車工業は挽回生産で持ち直し、AI関連需要で生産用機械工業も増産 
2026年 9月の生産指数は前月比＋2.2％と、コンセンサス（同＋1.2％、Bloomberg調査）を
上回り、3カ月ぶりに上昇した。経済産業省は基調判断を「一進一退」に据え置いた。 
生産指数を業種別に見ると、15業種中 8業種が前月から上昇した。自動車工業（前月比＋
5.5％）は 2カ月ぶりに上昇した。品目別では、普通乗用車（同＋3.3％）や駆動伝導・操縦装
置部品（同＋7.0％）などが増産となった。前月は熊本地震や台風による工場稼働停止や部品
供給の停滞を受けて生産が落ち込んだが、9 月は生産体制がおおむね正常化したことで挽回生
産が進み、自動車工業の生産は持ち直した。また、生産用機械工業（同＋3.4％）は4カ月連続
で上昇した。品目別では、AI関連製品である半導体製造装置（同＋13.8％）のほか、化学機械
（同＋34.9％）などが増産となった。経済産業省「鉱工業出荷内訳表」によると、生産用機械
工業は国内向け（同＋7.8％）が好調だった。汎用・業務用機械工業（同＋4.8％）では、コン
ベヤ（同＋142.0％）や運搬用クレーン（同＋35.5％）が増産となり、2カ月ぶりの上昇となっ
た。一方、鉄鋼・非鉄金属工業（前月比▲1.6％）や窯業・土石製品工業（同▲1.9％）など 7
業種は前月から低下した。鉄鋼・非鉄金属工業では亜鉛めっき鋼板（同▲10.0％）などが、窯
業・土石製品工業ではガラス繊維（短繊維）（同▲6.3％）などが減産となった。 
財別では、生産財（前月比＋1.0％）、耐久消費財（同＋5.3％）、非耐久消費財（同＋0.9％）、
建設財（同＋2.5％）、資本財（除．輸送機械） （同＋1.0％）のいずれも増産となった。設備投
資の関連指標である資本財（除．輸送機械）はAI需要を背景に大幅な増産が見込まれる。

【出荷・在庫】出荷指数は自動車工業などを中心に2カ月ぶりに上昇 
2026年 9月の出荷指数は前月比＋0.6％と 2カ月ぶりに上昇した。業種別では、自動車工業
（同＋2.3％）や汎用・業務用機械工業（同＋5.5％）など 15業種中 8業種が上昇した。財別に
見ると、非耐久消費財と資本財（除．輸送機械）、建設財は低下した一方、耐久消費財と生産
財は上昇した。在庫指数は同＋2.5％、在庫率指数は同＋2.0％だった。

生産予測調査による。

【先行き】生産指数は緩やかに上昇する見込み 
先行きの生産指数は、緩やかな上昇基調を辿るとみている。AI関連需要などが引き続き国内
生産の押し上げ要因となる。ただし、世界的な金利高、特に米国の金利高による海外需要の下
振れなどが生産を下押しするリスクには注意が必要だ。 
AI関連財の拡大は 2024年から日本国内の生産を押し上げてきており、今後も生産の押し上
げ要因になると見込まれる1。26年 8月の世界半導体販売額（3カ月平均）を見ると、前年比＋
144.3％と強い伸びが続いている。米国の販売額（同＋180.9％）がけん引する中、日本（同＋
62.3％）2も力強さを示している。また、日本半導体製造装置協会によると、26年9月の半導体
製造装置販売高（3カ月平均）は同＋48.2％と高い伸びとなった3。AI関連を中心とする旺盛な
半導体需要が反映されているとみられる。ただし、米国の金利高でAI関連需要が下振れする可
能性には注意が必要だ。高金利下で米企業の資金調達負担が増え、多額の資金を必要とする AI
関連投資が鈍化すれば4、日本の生産も輸出減を通して下押しされるリスクがある。 
なお、中東情勢による悪影響は引き続き注視する必要がある。米国の中間選挙後に対イラン
攻撃が再開される可能性も報じられており5、原油供給をめぐる不安は引き続き残る。一方、今
年 2月末に始まった中東危機を受け、ホルムズ海峡の輸送停滞への対応として政府が国家備蓄
原油を放出して以降、初めて買い戻しが実施された6。代替調達が進み、国家備蓄原油の取り崩
しが一服し、備蓄の補充が始まったことは好材料だ。ただし、情勢が再び悪化すれば、原材料
不足を通じて生産が下押しされる可能性がある。 
製造工業生産予測調査では、10月の生産指数は前月比＋5.1％と見込まれる。業種別では 11
業種中 9業種が上昇する見通しだ。電子部品・デバイス工業（同＋16.6％）で集積回路などの
増産が計画されているほか、生産用機械工業（同＋15.5％）では半導体・フラットパネルディ
スプレイ製造装置など、AI関連製品の増産が全体を押し上げる見通しだ。生産指数全体の計画
のバイアスを補正した試算値（最頻値）7で見ると、10月は同＋3.9％と上昇が見込まれる。 
11月の生産指数は前月比＋0.8％と見込まれている。業種別では 11業種中 7業種が上昇する
見通しだ。輸送機械工業（同＋4.3％）では乗用車など、汎用・業務用機械工業（同＋4.8％）
では計測・分析機器などの増産が見込まれている。

2026年6月23日）を参照。 
2 World Semiconductor Trade Statistics “Historical Billings Report”（最終閲覧日：2026年10月 30
日） 
3 日本半導体製造装置協会「2026年9月度販売高（SEAJ速報値）日本製半導体製造装置（3ヶ月平均）」
（2026年10月 23日）

【26年9月景気動向指数】先行CIは低下、一致CIは上昇を見込む 
鉱工業指数の結果を受けて、2026年 11月 9日に公表予定の 9月分の景気動向指数は、先行
CIが前月差▲0.9ptの 117.1、一致 CIが同＋0.7ptの 119.5と予想する（図表 4） 。先行CIで
は構成指標のうち、鉱工業用生産財在庫率指数（逆サイクル）や最終需要財在庫率指数（逆サ
イクル）、東証株価指数などが前月から悪化した。一致 CIでは構成指標のうち、生産指数（鉱
工業）や鉱工業用生産財出荷指数、有効求人倍率（除学卒）などが改善した。この予測値に基
づくと、2026年9月の基調判断は機械的に「改善」に据え置かれる。

Update
King Slide Works Co. Ltd.  | Asia Pacific
October Sales +2% MoM/+191%
YoY
In this report, we focus on King Slide's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price movement.
Details::
• October sales were NT$4,562mn (+2% MoM/+191% YoY)
Our view:
• We think the monthly sales run rate in the near term should stay roughly at
the Sep-26 level and might start to tick up from late 4Q26/early 1Q27 along
with the new server platform ramp.

• Near-term softness should be well known and dilution from the recently
announced CB issuance is manageable (up to 1.4% dilution based on the
latest price), while the growth outlook still looks promising for 2027 amid
the increasing penetration of rail kits into more hardware devices in AI
clusters.
• We maintain our view that King Slide's competitiveness in the comprehensive
patent portfolio and strong execution are entrenched and accentuated amid

fast generation migration in AI applications.
• Stay OW.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
King Slide Works Co. Ltd. (2059.TW)
Base case, 30x 2027e P/E. We use price/earnings as our valuation methodology because we
believe it better captures King Slide's earnings growth potential. We believe our target mul-
tiple is justified by an operating profit CAGR of 77% seen for 2025-28, implying a PEG of 0.4
vs. the average of 1.2 over 2010-24, when the stock was trading at 17-18x P/E with an operating

profit CAGR of 14%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected AI capex
n Stronger server demand
n Faster market share gains
Risks to Downside
n Softer AI capex
n Weaker server demand
n Sluggish kitchen furniture demand
n Competition leading to slower share gains.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

buy recommendation; we correspond Equal-weight and Not-Rated to hold and Underweight to sell recommendations, respectively.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

## Document 174: 261110_daiwa_hon-precision-buy-initiation

Source PDF: all report pdf/261110_daiwa_hon-precision-buy-initiation.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-10
Pages: 23
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

Investment case: We initiate coverage of Hon Precision, the global leader 
in test handlers and active thermal control (ATC) systems, with a Buy (1) 
rating. According to management, the company commands >70% market 
share in AI/HPC final test (FT) and c.50% in system-level test (SLT) by 
revenue, distinctly positioning it as one of the key global beneficiaries of the 
rising silicon complexity.

Multi-layer content stacking supports ASP and margin growth. As test 
durations continue to lengthen and thermal requirements rise, customers 
are required to deploy greater parallel testing capacity and higher-
specification handlers, creating a structural demand tailwind for both FT 
and SLT handler. We forecast rising AI chip power consumption and 
package complexity over our forecast horizon to drive adoption of higher-
value ATC systems, mega-tray handlers (adding 25-30% to ASP relative to 
standard dual-temp ATC systems), and AOI integration (adding a further 
10-15% to system ASP), supporting a multi-year increase in content per 
tool and expanding its gross margin from 56.8% to 59.2% over 2026-28E.

Recurring consumables and CPO testing expand long-term growth 
visibility. Cold plates generate high-margin, annuity-like revenue with 
every new chip platform which should benefit from shortening AI processor 
refresh cycles. Beyond consumables, Hon Precision is extending its testing 
platform into co-packaged optics (CPO), where optical-electrical co-testing 
could unlock a new addressable market beyond traditional GPU and ASIC 
testing. While near-term contribution remains limited, we view CPO as a 
strategic growth option that enhances the company’s long-term earnings 
visibility and valuation premium over our forecast horizon.

Catalysts: 1) capacity expansion ramp in 2027; 2) mega-tray penetration 
and higher-power ATC adoption; 3) initial CPO test solution qualification.

Valuation: We initiate with a Buy (1) rating and 12M TP of TWD6,365, 
based on a target PER of 30x applied on our 2027E EPS. The stock 
currently trades at c.19x forward PER, despite our projected 2026-28E EPS 
CAGR of 55%, implying a PEG of only 0.35x. We believe its current 
valuation heavily discounts macro noise with share price pulling back by 
c.53% from its historical high and derating from a cycle-peak multiple of 45-
50x 1-year-forward PER. Backed by solid order visibility through late-2027, 
we highlight Hon Precision’s risk-reward profile as decisively asymmetric.

Risks: 1) Slower-than-expected AI/HPC investment; 2) delayed ramp-up of 
new products; and 3) potential loss of market share.

10 November 2026

Hon Precision

➢ Recurring revenue and CPO set to unlock new TAM expansion

Growth outlook

Hon Precision: revenue and gross margin outlook 
We expect Hon Precision’s growth momentum over 2026-
28E to be driven by increasing testing content per chip, 
supported by rising power consumption in AI/HPC chips, 
larger package sizes, and growing system complexity. We 
expect the transition towards high-end ATC handlers, 
mega-tray solutions, and integrated AOI functions to drive 
meaningful ASP expansion over our forecast horizon, with 
recurring cold plate demand providing an additional gross-
margin growth driver.

Source: Company, Daiwa research

Valuation   Hon Precision: 1-year-forward PER bands 
Our 12-month TP of TWD6,365 is based on a target PER 
of 30x applied to our 2027E EPS forecast. Following the 
strong share-price rally in 1H26, investor focus shifted 
away from Hon Precision due to limited near-term catalysts 
ahead of the 2H27 ramp-up of mega-tray handlers, CPO 
testing solutions and new capacity. We believe the recent 
53% pullback from its historical high on 29 May 2026 and 
derating from 45-50x 1-year-forward PER to c.19x more 
than prices in the macro uncertainties. At the current levels, 
the stock offers an attractive risk-reward profile, trading at 
a PEG of just 0.35x based on our 2026-28E EPS CAGR of 
55%.

Source: Bloomberg

Hon Precision: Bloomberg-consensus earnings revisions 
The Bloomberg consensus raised its 2026E and 2027E

past year, driven by stronger-than-expected AI-related 
demand and improving visibility on the company’s long-
term growth trajectory. We believe the upward revisions 
reflect rising confidence in volume and ASP expansion 
across the company’s key testing solutions, supported by 
accelerating AI infrastructure investments and expanding 
customer adoption.

Company profile 
Founded in 1999, Hon Precision is the global leader in semiconductor test handlers and active thermal 
control (ATC) systems. The company dominates the global AI/HPC final test handler market, supported 
by its advanced thermal management technologies. With over 70% market share in AI/HPC test 
handling, Hon Precision benefits from growing demand for AI, HPC and custom ASIC applications. Its 
solutions are widely adopted by leading OSATs and foundries worldwide, making it a key enabler of 
advanced semiconductor testing.

### Page 6

6

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026 
Growth Catalysts 
Multi-factor dollar content stacking 
We believe the primary growth catalyst for Hon Precision over our forecast horizon stems 
from multi-layered dollar content stacking, where the rise in machine unit value should 
outpace volume growth. Next-gen AI accelerators have historically exhibited more than 50-
70% increase in thermal design power (TDP) per generation, with platforms like Nvidia 
Rubin and Rubin Ultra surging to 2.3kW and 3.6kW vs. Blackwell’s 1.2kW to 1.4kW. 
Managing these extreme thermal loads makes high-end active thermal control (ATC) a 
necessity, driving a structural step-up in ASPs from standard handlers at approximately 
TWD6m to dual-temperature ATC systems at TWD12-15m, and tri-temperature ATC 
systems at TWD15-18m.

Concurrently, as the package size of next-generation ASICs/GPUs increases from the 
current 120mm tray specification to around 150mm, Hon Precision is introducing mega-
tray large-package solutions in October 2026, which command a 25-30% ASP premium 
over mainstream GPU handlers. As the majority of next-generation ASIC/GPU is set to 
exceed the boundary of 120mm, we estimate shipment penetration for mega-tray solutions 
will likely reach 40% in 2027E. Integrated functions of Automated Optical Inspection (AOI) 
will contribute an additional 10-15% ASP uplift to unit value of handler. Furthermore, this 
content expansion extends into the flagship mobile AP market as smartphone processors 
transition to multi-die packaging. For example, Apple’s A20 Pro processor has already 
adopted wafer-level multi-chip module (WMCM) packaging in 2026, requiring high-end ATC 
systems capable of managing up to 3,000W thermal dissipation to resolve device self-
heating.

Hon Precision: ASP multipliers

Source: Daiwa research

High-margin cold plate consumables  
In addition to capital equipment set sales, Hon Precision’s cold plate consumables 
business serves as a crucial recurring revenue engine and a primary driver of gross margin 
expansion. Cold plates carry the highest gross margin across all company product lines. 
Driven by the compression of AI processor lifecycles to 1-1.5 years (down from 2-3 years 
historically), each test handler requires 5-10 distinct, custom-engineered cold plates 
tailored to specific die footprints, socket layouts, and thermal hot-spot geometries. To 
conduct parallel test and maintain the throughput of chip testing, test handler for system 
level test (SLT) requires even more cold plates, which can go up to 24 plates. As a result, 
cold plate tooling generates high-frequency annuity-like sales that recur with every new 
chip platform iteration, independent of major equipment replacements.

Hon Precision: cold plate revenue trend

Testing duration and volume multiples 
We believe the rapid rise in AI chip complexity is driving structural growth in semiconductor 
testing intensity, positioning Hon Precision as a key global beneficiary through its 
leadership in high-parallelism test handlers. Driven by the mainstream adoption of 
heterogeneous integration (such as TSMC’s CoWoS and Intel’s EMIB), verifying complex 
AI silicon has extended test durations by 2x to 10x vs. standard consumer chips (5 to 10 
seconds). Final test durations for AI GPUs have expanded from hundreds of seconds to 
over 10 minutes per chip. To maintain daily manufacturing throughput despite this 10x 
spike, chipmakers must scale up massive parallel testing architectures and procure 
significantly more handlers, cementing Hon Precision as the primary test handler partner 
for tier-one US AI GPU and CPU players.

Concurrently, escalating chip complexity, TDP, and die costs make field return (RMA) 
penalties far exceed testing expenses, forcing cloud service providers (CSPs) and 
automotive OEMs to mandate system-level testing (SLT). Because SLT executes over 
extended durations ranging from minutes to hours per chip, high-volume production 
mandates automated parallel SLT handlers equipped with high-capacity ATC. Holding 
c.50% market share in AI/HPC SLT handlers alongside Chroma, Hon Precision is 
expanding beyond GPUs into custom CSP ASICs (eg, Google TPU and AWS) and server 
CPUs. As SLT adoption broadens across non-GPU devices, Hon Precision leverages this 
multi-year runway to transition into a full-spectrum semiconductor testing platform.

Global: growing chip complexity – testing time by AI/HPC chip

Source: Daiwa research

CPO unlocks new TAM beyond GPUs 
Beyond conventional electrical testing, Co-Packaged Optics (CPO) represents a multi-year 
paradigm shift towards a multimodal optical-electrical testing platform. Hon Precision 
currently monetises initial CPO deployments by supplying Final Test (FT) handlers for pure 
electrical testing (E/E test) of switch ASICs and Optical Engines (OEs) on production 
platforms such as Nvidia’s Spectrum-X and Quantum-X.

### Page 8

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026

However, the strategic growth catalyst is anchored in next-generation Insertion 4 (package-
level optical and electrical co-testing, O/E co-test). Because an undetected defect in a 
single optical component would force manufacturers to scrap an entire high-value CPO 
module, early and rigorous package-level validation is imperative for yield protection. 
Collaborating directly with ATE leader Teradyne and optical instrument specialists (such as 
Keysight), Hon Precision is developing a specialised ATC handler capable of simultaneous 
optical and electrical validation, including Bit Error Rate (BER), optical loopback, crosstalk, 
and high-power thermal management testing for integrated ASIC and Optical Engine 
modules in a single touchdown.

Engaged with Nvidia CPO switch programmes and actively expanding to additional ASIC 
clients, Hon Precision targets engineering verification in late-2026 and initial shipments in 
2027, ahead of 2028 volume production. Although we expect revenue contribution will 
likely be relatively low (<5% in 2027-2028), it drives valuation and market expectation to 
serve larger total addressable market (TAM).

Market Analysis 
Global testing equipment market 
According to TechInsights, global testing equipment capex is projected to expand 
significantly from USD8.3bn in 2024 to USD26.1bn by 2027E, representing a 46.4% 
CAGR. Notably, while SoC test systems front-loaded strong growth in 2024-25, before 
tapering to 9.6% YoY in 2027E on TechInsights estimates, package handling equipment is 
forecasted to exhibit strong structural resilience and longevity. Based on TechInsights, 
market capex for package handling equipment will likely more than double from USD941m 
in 2024 to USD2.6bn by 2027E (40.9% CAGR), re-accelerating to 46% YoY growth in 
2027E from 34% YoY in 2025. This cadence reflects the phased deployment of test 
infrastructure – initial tester investments are followed by high-volume back-end handling 
installations as advanced packaging (eg, CoWoS, Chiplets) scales into mass production.

Global semi testing equipment: capex  Global semi testing equipment: handler and prober growth

Source: T echInsights, Daiwa research  Source: T echInsights, Daiwa research

Handler market players 
Hon Precision’s overall share in the global test handler TAM rose from 35% in 2024 to 57% 
in 2025, and we forecast this to reach 75% in 2026E and 79% by 2027E. Within the high-
value AI and HPC Final Test (FT) handler segment, Hon Precision maintains a dominant 
market share, exceeding 70% in both sales value and shipment volume, per management. 
In the rapidly expanding AI and HPC SLT handler segment, Hon Precision holds c.50% 
market share in terms of revenue, per management, alongside its primary peer, Chroma, 
establishing a duopoly in global high-end SLT testing.

Hon Precision: handler market share forecasts

Source: Daiwa research

and regional vendors, including Cohu (COHU US, NR), Advantest (6857 JP, JPY28,375, 
Outperform [2]; covered by Daiwa analyst Takeshi Fukuyama), and mainland Chinese 
suppliers such as Changchuan Technology (300604 CH, NR) and JHT Design (603061

### Page 10

10

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026

While global incumbents rely on brand prestige, standard architectures, and bundled 
offerings, Hon Precision excels in leading-edge AI/HPC testing through its ultra-high-power 
Active Thermal Control (ATC) and rapid engineering turnaround. As multi-kilowatt dynamic 
loads (1.5kW–3kW+) and extreme warpage on >120mm packages push standard handlers 
beyond their limits, Hon Precision’s hyper-customised ATC fluidics, high-force micro-
mechanics, and modular designs deliver superior millisecond thermal clamping and agile 
package adaptation. Furthermore, while giants like Advantest dominate the SoC ATE tester 
market (>55% share), their in-house handlers focus primarily on memory and standard 
logic. Consequently, tier-one chipmakers and OSATs standardly decouple tester and 
handler sourcing, thus pairing Advantest or Teradyne testers with Hon Precision’s high-
power ATC handlers. Located directly within Taiwan’s advanced packaging cluster, Hon 
Precision provides real-time, on-site co-validation and custom cold-plate engineering that 
preserve formidable entry barriers against bundled solutions.

Meanwhile, although Chinese competitors have expanded aggressively in domestic mature 
nodes and conventional Pick-and-Place handlers, Hon Precision commands a decisive 
generational lead in high-complexity advanced packaging applications. Chinese vendors 
remain largely confined to lower-wattage consumer, automotive, and industrial IC testing, 
lacking the proven patents, structural rigidity, and micro-level force balancing required to 
safely apply hundreds of kilograms of contact force onto fragile, multi-chip CoWoS 
packages without cracking dies or warping substrates. Moreover, because enterprise AI 
chips cost tens of thousands of dollars each, tier-1 global fabless leaders and North 
American cloud service providers (CSPs) maintain virtually zero tolerance for testing 
failure, establishing an entrenched validation moat for Hon Precision. Free from 
geopolitical export constraints, Hon Precision can seamlessly deploy across global OSAT 
networks in Taiwan, North America, and Southeast Asia.

### Page 11

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026 
Financial Analysis 
AI demand to sustain strong revenue growth 
In 2025, Hon Precision’s top line exceeded TWD30bn (+116% YoY), driven by global AI 
cluster rollouts. We forecast Hon Precision to sustain its top-line growth momentum over 
2026-28E. We expect this to be mainly driven by core test handler and ATC systems (70-
75% of revenue), where we forecast capacity to expand 40% YoY in 2026E, and 50% YoY 
over 2027-28E with utilisation rate remaining above 90%. We forecast blended ASP of test

margin cold plate consumables revenue should also benefit from test handler shipment, 
which we forecast to increase at a 66.1% CAGR over 2025-28E.

Balance sheet and cash flow 
Driven by surging AI/HPC testing demand, Hon Precision is actively expanding its capacity 
across the new Desheng facility, localised production at the Changshu site, and 
debottlenecking of headquarter efficiency. The company targets 40-50% capacity growth in 
2026 and another 50% increase in 2027. The expansion of manufacturing space and 
production facilities requires an increase in capital expenditure, which we project Hon 
Precision will ramp up to approximately TWD2.8bn in 2026E, TWD3.8bn in 2027E, and 
TWD4.6bn in 2028E. The company’s operating cash inflows are more than sufficient to 
fully fund factory expansions alongside a c.70% dividend payout ratio.

Hon Precision continues to maintain an un-levered balance sheet with zero financial bank 
debt. Cash and cash equivalents exceeded TWD53bn in 2025; we project this figure to 
surpass TWD75bn by 2028E. Its robust balance sheet underpins peer-leading shareholder 
returns, with ROE to expand from 34.5% in 2025 to 59.6% by 2028E. This self-funding 
cash engine supports management’s targeted c.70% dividend payout ratio.

Valuation and recommendation 
Initiating coverage with a Buy (1) rating 
We initiate coverage of Hon Precision with a Buy (1) rating and 12-month TP of TWD6,365, 
based on a target PER of 30x applied to our 2027E EPS forecast. When the stock rallied 
above TWD8,000 in 1H26, its forward PER multiple stretched to a cycle peak of 45-50x. 
However, we expect key growth catalysts, including a 50% aggregate capacity expansion, 
alongside higher-ASP shipments of large-form-factor mega tray handlers and co-packaged 
optics (CPO) test solutions, to drive its top line, primarily in 2H27. With existing production 
lines operating at full utilisation, investors are concerned over a potential temporary 
deceleration in growth momentum through 2H26-1H27, ahead of the next potential major 
capacity inflection in 2H27.

However, as the calendar rolls into 2027, we believe market attention will swiftly refocus on 
the company’s multi-year structural thesis. Easing capacity bottlenecks, combined with 
robust ASIC-driven adoption of mega tray architectures and ramping CPO test platforms, 
should spark a powerful volume-and-ASP expansion cycle. The stock is currently trading at 
a 19x 1-year-forward PER vs. its earnings CAGR of 55% over 2026-28E, translating into a 
0.35x PEG ratio. We believe the valuation now heavily discounts the near-term pause in 
revenue and macro noises, presenting a compelling risk-reward profile for long-term 
investors.

Risks to our call 
1. Slower-than-expected AI/HPC investment: A slowdown in AI infrastructure spending 
or delays in GPU, ASIC and server CPU ramps could reduce demand for high-end 
FT/SLT handlers and thermal-control solutions, weakening order growth and revenue 
visibility. 
2. Slower-than-expected new product adoption: Customer qualification delays or 
slower adoption of mega-tray handlers, AOI inspection systems and CPO FT solutions 
could delay product ramps, resulting in revenue growth below our expectations. 
3. Potential share loss: Competitors’ advances in thermal control, customer qualification 
of alternative suppliers or delays in meeting new testing requirements could weaken 
Hon Precision’s position on next-generation platforms, affecting market share and 
pricing power.

### Page 15

15

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026 
Appendix 
Company introduction 
Hon Precision was founded in Taichung in 2015, building on the technology and industry 
experience of Hon Technology, which was established in 1999. The company develops 
semiconductor test handlers and thermal-control solutions for AI GPUs, AI ASICs, server 
CPUs, automotive chips and consumer electronics, supporting customers from new-
product introduction (NPI) through volume production.

Hon Precision has two main product categories:

• Semiconductor test handlers: Final-test (FT) and system-level-test (SLT) handlers 
automate chip loading, positioning and sorting. These platforms can incorporate active 
thermal control (ATC) systems to maintain chips at specified temperatures during 
testing. 
• Testing fixtures and modules: Mainly customised cold plates and socket layout kits 
(SLKs), which provide thermal management and mechanical interfaces for different chip 
packages. Customers may require replacement cold plates and kits as package designs 
change, even when retaining existing handlers.

Hon Precision’s order mix is significantly weighted towards AI/HPC/ASIC applications, 
which accounted for 78% of orders in 1Q-3Q26, up from 72% in 2025. These applications 
include AI computing, servers and networking. Automotive represented 9% of orders, 
followed by mobile AP and communications at 7%, consumer electronics at 4%, and 
memory and MEMS at 2%.

As for capacity, Hon Precision’s manufacturing footprint is primarily based in Taiwan, with 
Taichung serving as the group’s main R&D, engineering, quality management, and 
equipment production hub. In addition, the company operates a Suzhou facility focused on 
manufacturing cold plates and related kits, while Taichung Plant 4 is expected to provide 
incremental production capacity to support future growth.

Shareholder structure 
Hon Precision’s major shareholders include several investment and development 
companies. As of 17 April 2026, Hung Min Development was the largest shareholder with a 
17.37% stake, followed by Hongji Investment at 12.08% and Hong Yu Development at 
7.80%. Chairman Wen-Ta Hsieh also directly held 1.77%. International institutional 
shareholders included Norges Bank and GIC, with disclosed stakes of 1.30% and 0.97%, 
respectively. The company has a free float of 48%, allowing broad public-market 
participation and supporting trading liquidity.

### Page 17

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026 
Appendix 
What is handler? 
A semiconductor test handler automates the movement, positioning and temperature 
conditioning of packaged chips during testing. It places each device into a test socket, 
applies the required contact force and sorts the device according to the test results. The 
tester performs the electrical measurements, while the handler ensures consistent 
mechanical and thermal conditions.

The process begins with packaged chips loaded into the handler in trays. After temperature 
conditioning, the handler transfers each chip into a test socket connected to the tester 
through a load board. Once electrical contact is established, the tester applies signals and 
measures the chip’s response. For ATC-equipped systems, a customised cold plate works 
with the active thermal control system to maintain the required chip temperature 
throughout testing. The handler then unloads and sorts the chips based on their test 
results.

Semiconductor test handler: workflow and thermal control system

Source: Hon Precision, Daiwa research

Handlers are used in both final test (FT) and system-level test (SLT). FT handlers work 
with automated test equipment (ATE) to check electrical characteristics and functionality 
against specifications. SLT handlers place chips into test positions connected to system-
level platforms, typically comprising test boards, test PCs and power supplies, to evaluate 
operation under conditions resembling actual use.

### Page 18

18

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026 
Semiconductor back-end testing FT workflow

Source: Semiconductor engineering

Thermal control becomes increasingly important as AI/HPC chips consume more power 
and adopt larger, more complex packages. Different test patterns change power 
consumption and heat distribution, requiring dynamic heating or cooling to keep the chip 
within a specified temperature range. Precise handling and temperature control support 
repeatable measurements, while parallel testing and efficient chip movement help improve 
overall test throughput.

We hosted NYPCB’s 3Q26 post result on November 9th. Key 
takeaways include: 
(1) NYPCB mentioned its 4Q26 GM on ABF substrate already 
exceeds 40% (vs. ~15% in 2025 and 25% in 1H26) driven mainly by 
the solid high-end product demand (which contributed 50%+ of the 
ABF revenue in 2026 vs. <20% in 2025), while the company 
mentioned it will gain more high-end market share in 2027 which 
should further drive company’s overall GM level.  
(2) For the new high-end projects in 2027, NYPCB mentioned it will 
gain significant share in the key US CSP next-gen AI project, and 
high-end server CPU project. Also, the company mentioned it 
expects a proactive adoption on 1.6T Ethernet switch demand 
driven by CSPs investment in AI infrastructure, driving its high-end 
switch substrate ASP (the upgrade on substrate, from 800G to 1.6T, 
suggesting a 50%+ higher substrate unit price, per GSe).  
(3) In terms of the ABF substrate pricing, NYPCB believes its ABF 
substrate ASP will further go up by 10-15% QoQ in 4Q26, while it 
also expects the pricing hike ratio to be at least maintained at this 
level from 1Q27-4Q27, without seeing much downside risk. 
Moreover, the company believes the ASP growth could be even 
faster than its expectation if the high-end orders accelerate in 
coming quarters or if they see an even tigher S/D outlook for the 
overall ABF substrate industry.  
(4) In terms of the BT substrate pricing outlook, the company 
mentioned the pricing hike ratio will be low single digits % QoQ in 
4Q26, which will further trend up in 1Q27-4Q27, with a high 
confidence level on the shortage of BT substrate in coming quarters.  
(4) In terms of 4Q26 revenue guidance, the company is expecting 
~20% QoQ revenue growth, driven mainly by the high-end ABF 
projects ramp up + new AI project ramps + BT substrate pricing hike

(low single digits % QoQ); moreover, the company expects its 1Q27 revenue to be flat 
QoQ, despite the ~20% less working days. For GM, the company expects 4Q26 GM to be 
low to mid 30% which should further improve in 1Q27.  
(5) The company mentioned it is working on a new capacity investment plan for 2028, 
but has not yet finalized the plan. In terms of the funding method, NYPCB mentioned it 
will not do any equity based fundraising, but will rely on internal cash or bank loan.  
(6) NYPCB also mentioned it will focus only on CoPoS technology, whose substrate 
pricing is also US$1000+ (vs. US$100-200 for the most high end ABF substrate now), 
while the product demand could start ramping up from 2028 and start contributing 
significant revenue from 2029. For EMIB-T, NYPCB believes the penetration rate could 
only be 10% in the foreseeable future, and it has no plan to invest in this small market.  
We maintain our positive view on the overall ABF substrate industry, with 34%/51% 
shortage ratio in 2027/28 (see here), and believe such high shortage ratio will protect 
companies’ GM/OPM even if we start to see some slowdown on AI CAPEX in coming 
years. We started to see all ABF substrate players’ GM surpass 40% in 4Q26, which is 
much faster than our original expectation (Taiwan ABF substrate suppliers reach 40%+ 
GM at the 6-7th quarters of the shortage in last upcycle, but it only takes 3-4 quarters 
for Taiwan players to hit the 40%+ GM, driven mainly by the solid AI upgrading demand 
this time). We expect NYPCB’s ABF substrate GM could reach historical peak level 
(55-60%) in 2H27, suggesting a fruitful upside for the EPS growth in coming years (we 
expect NYPCB’s EPS to grow by 150%+ 2025-29E CAGR), and believe the ABF substrate 
will continue to be a key bottleneck of the overall semi/tech supply chain in the next 3-5 
years.  
Maintain Buy on NYPCB, as the key spot price ABF substrate supplier in the market 
which should benefit strongly from the S/D shortage and pricing hike in the 
foreseeable future, with an unchanged TP of NT$2,700 (based on 21x 2028E EPS).  
Investment Thesis & PT methodology and risks

Investment Thesis - NYPCB 
NYPCB manufactures and markets printed circuit boards (PCBs) and integrated circuit 
(IC) substrates (including ABF and BT substrates). We are Buy rated on NYPCB 
considering the company’s high exposure to BT substrates and non-LTA ABF substrate 
(70%+ of total revenue), where we expect GM/OPM to increase significantly in the 
coming quarters (from breakeven in OPM in 1H25 to 26/42% in 2026/27E) due to the 
favorable substrate pricing outlook. Despite the company’s low exposure to high-end 
ABF substrate industry (<20% of total revenue in 2025), we believe the expanding 
demand from ASIC AI server and high-end switch IC demand will drive the company’s 
high-end ABF revenue exposure to 40%+ by 2027E, suggesting better GM/OPM outlook 
in the long term. In 2H25-2H26, we believe NYPCB will continue to benefit from stronger 
ABF/BT pricing and should be a key beneficiary of the pricing uptrend considering the 
company’s key focus on BT and non-LTA ABF substrate industries. Moreover, in the long 
term (after 2026), we expect the ABF industry to return to supply tightness, and NYPCB’s 
expanding high-end exposure will lead to a long-term earnings growth outlook (148% 
2026-28E earnings CAGR). As a result, we see solid upside potential for NYPCB’s

Slower-than-expected ABF substrates pricing upgrading outlook, and (3)
Slower-than-expected NYPCB new high-end capacity qualification progress.

on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

文曄(3036) 
26Q4資料中心恢復成長  
投資評等 目標價 
逢低買進 250 
前次投資建議 2026.8.6： 
「逢低買進」，目標價：$300

報告日期：2026.11.6 研 究 員：林秀泓

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 26Q4 資料中心產品恢復強勁季增，通訊、資料中心產品受益 AI需求。 
➢ 2026 營收翻倍，2027營收增速減緩。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/11/5 收盤價： 195.00 ➢ 26Q3 營收 5,973 億元，QoQ+1.1%，YoY+81.6%，符合
財測 QoQ-3.2%~+3.6%。毛利率 3.60%(QoQ+0.19pt，
YoY-0.14pt)，符合財測 3.5%~3.6%，主要因為產品組合
改善。EPS 7.97元。 
➢ 26Q4 文曄財測以美元匯率 32.2 估計，預估營收
7,300~7,770 億元(QoQ+22.2%~+30.1%)，主要由資料中
心出貨恢復帶動。毛利率 3.2%~3.3%。營業費用
7,154~7,546 億元。財測 EPS 8.07~9.05 元。 
➢ 展望 26Q4 各應用別(以美元估計)，汽車電子受提前拉貨
影響，預期季減雙位數百分比；工業與儀器因季節性因
素，預期季減中個位數百分比；通訊受益於 AI 光通訊需
求，預期季增高雙位數百分比；資料中心與伺服器預期季
增高雙位數百分比，全年成長約兩倍；消費性產品因提前
拉貨，預期季減中雙位數百分比；PC 預期季減；手機旺
季提前拉貨後，預期季減低雙位數百分比。 
➢ 調整預估 2026/2027 年營收 24,151 億元/27,170 億元，
YoY+105.0%/+12.5%，EPS 28.85 元/33.57 元。 
➢ 目前股價處於 6X 27(F) PER，為過去 PE區間 7~40X的
低位。考量 2026 年 AI 帶動營收大幅成長，2027 年 YoY
增速明顯減緩，投資評等維持「逢低買進」 ，調整目標價
250 元(7X 27(F) PER)，潛在上漲空間 28%。

簡 要 財 務 預 估 資 訊

營 運 分 析

文曄為 IC通路代理商 
文曄成立於 1993/12 成立，2002/08 上市掛牌，為提供全球專業電子零組件
通路服務的廠商，透過合併多家 IC通路商的方式持續擴大公司規模。營運總
部設立於台灣，目前在中國、韓國、新加坡、印度、泰國、馬來西亞、越南
等地區共有五十多個營運據點。代理半導體原廠超過 400 家，服務客戶超過
25,000 家。根據 2025 年 Gartner 最新數據，文曄已經成為全球最大和亞太
區最大的半導體通路商，市佔分別為 15.1%和 17.0%。 
產品組合方面，以代理產品的應用劃分營收，26Q3 汽車電子佔 5%、工業與
儀器佔 9%、通訊佔 13%、資料中心及伺服器佔 56%、消費性及其他佔 5%、
個人電腦與週邊佔 4%、手機佔 9%。

- 3 - 
26Q3 財報符合財測，獲利再創單季新高 
26Q3 營收 5,973 億元，QoQ+1.1%，YoY+81.6%，符合財測 5,720~6,120
億元(QoQ-3.2%~+3.6%)。毛利率 3.60%(QoQ+0.19pt，YoY-0.14pt)，符合
財測 3.5%~3.6%，主要因為高毛利的工業、車用、Future 營收季增，產品組
合改善。營業費用QoQ+1.6%，費用率1.22%維持低檔，營業槓桿效益持續。
營業利益 142.3 億元，QoQ+9.5%，YoY+151.5%，優於財測中位數 140 億
元。業外方面，淨財務成本約19.4 億元，高於原先預估，因為資金成本上升。
其他業外收入 5.8 億元主要來自權益法認列日電貿的獲利，以及股利收入。
淨利 103.0 億元，QoQ+6.0%，YoY+169.9%，EPS 7.97元。

- 4 - 
26Q4 營收預估季增，資料中心恢復成長 
26Q4 文曄財測以美元匯率 32.2 估計，預估營收 7,300~7,770 億元
(QoQ+22.2%~+30.1%，YoY+113.4%~+127.1%)，主要由資料中心出貨恢復
帶動 。 毛利率3.2%~3.3%。 營業費用7,154~7,546億元 。 財測EPS 8.07~9.05
元。

26Q4 資料中心恢復高雙位數季增，通訊持續成長 
26Q3 分產品線來看(以新台幣計)，汽車電子 QoQ+18%，優於前次法說預期
季減中低個位數百分比；工業與儀器 QoQ+13%，優於預期季增個位數百分
比；通訊 QoQ+5%，符合預期；資料中心與伺服器 QoQ-5%、YoY+114%，
符合預期；消費性產品 QoQ-7%，符合預期；PC週邊 QoQ持平，優於預期
季減個位數百分比，若不含記憶體則QoQ-11%、YoY-20%；手機QoQ+32%，
符合預期，受惠旺季與提前拉貨。 
展望 26Q4 各應用別(以美元估計)，汽車電子：受提前拉貨及庫存調整影響，
預期季減雙位數百分比，YoY+20%以上，全年YoY+30%以上；工業與儀器：
季節性因素，預期季減中個位數百分比，YoY+40%以上，全年成長約40%；
通訊：受益於 AI光通訊需求，預期季增高雙位數百分比，全年成長約一倍；
資料中心與伺服器：出貨節奏調整後恢復，預期季增高雙位數百分比，全年
成長約兩倍，2027 年預期維持高速成長；消費性產品：因提前拉貨，預期季
減中雙位數百分比；PC：預期季減；手機：旺季提前拉貨後，預期季減低雙
位數百分比。

半導體市場成長再度上修 
CSP 的 AI 資本支出展望正向，AI 需求強且具結構性。根據 Gartner 數據，
2026 年全球半導體市場由原先預估 YoY+92%再度上修到 YoY+108%，2027
年預估 YoY+39.2%(不含記憶體+33%)。半導體市場 2025~2029 年 CAGR
由 25.9%上修到 35.1%，2029 年市場規模預估達 2.7 兆美元。data center
為成長最強勁的領域，2026/2027 年預估 YoY+171%/+60%，2025~2029 年
CAGR+54.9%。 
文曄 26Q3 資料中心受出貨節奏影響，26Q4 恢復強勁季增，2026 全年成長
約兩倍，2027 年預期維持高速成長；通訊受益於 AI 光通訊需求，成長動能
延續。Future 方面，歐美市場持續正向復甦，26Q3 營收 YoY+40%以上，車
用與歐洲工廠自動化已無去庫存壓力，資料中心業務也開始接單出貨。Future
較高毛利率可部分抵銷 AI 產品線低毛利率的影響。調整預估 2026/2027 年
營收 24,151 億元/27,170 億元，YoY+105.0%/+12.5%，淨利 387 億元/450
億元，YoY+185.9%/+16.3%，EPS 28.85 元/33.57 元。

目前股價處於 6X 27(F) PER，為過去 PE區間 7~40X的低位。考量 2026 年
AI 帶動營收大幅成長，2027 年 YoY 增速明顯減緩，投資評等維持「逢低買
進」 ，調整目標價 250 元(7X 27(F) PER)，潛在上漲空間 28%。

## Document 234: [005380] 현대차(005380) 저점을 받쳐줄 실적현대차(005380) 저점을 받쳐줄 실적저점을 받쳐줄 실적 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005380] 현대차(005380) 저점을 받쳐줄 실적현대차(005380) 저점을 받쳐줄 실적저점을 받쳐줄 실적 - 기업.pdf

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지 
6개월 
목표주가 500,000 
하향  
현재주가 
(26.10.29) 
292,500 
자동차업종

◼ 3Q26 비용절감/환율 효과로 우려 대비 양호한 실적 시현 
◼ 2027년 물량–환율 영향 상쇄되며 2H27 이익체력 개선 본격화 예상 
◼ 1) Physical AI(로봇/SDV) 경쟁력, 2) 주요국 점유율 반등에 주목

투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 50만원 하향(-6%) 
현대차 TP 50만원(시총 102조원)은 12MF EPS 41,978원에 Target PER 12배
적용하여 산출. 제한적인 실적 기대 반영해 타겟 밸류 하향함에 따라 TP 조정  
우려 대비 양호한 3Q26 실적으로 주가 하방 부담 완화. 주가의 추세적 상승 
위해서는 1) Physical AI(로봇/SDV) 경쟁력, 2) 주요국 점유율 반등 확인돼야 할 
것. 기저효과에 따른 물량 개선이 주가 저점 받쳐줄 것이나, 현재 밸류(2027년 
PER 9.1배vs 글로벌 피어 6.1배)에서 추세 상승 이끌긴 어려울 것이기 때문. 
이에, 자동차 본업 외 1) 한/미국 Physical AI 투자 계획 및 2) BD 공급망 구축 
/양산 가시화에 따른 주가 반등 기대

3Q26 매출 45조(YoY -3%, 이하 YoY), OP 2.7조(+6%), OPM 5.9% 시현 
3Q26 OP 추정(2.5조)/컨센(2.4조) 상회. 파업에 따른 생산/판매 차질 영향으로 
기대 제한적이었으나, 비용 절감/환율 영향으로 우려 대비 양호한 실적 시현. 
[자동차] 매출 33조(-10%), OP 2.1조(+25%), OPM 6.5%. 도매 판매 92.1만대
(-11%). 판매 부진과 원자재 부담에도 불구, 환효과(+1.3조)/비용절감(+0.5조)
로 실적 방어. 이외 YoY 변수별 영향은 물량(-1.1조)/믹스(-0.6조)/원자재(-
0.5조). [금융] 매출 9.2조(+28%), OP 4,710억(-18%), OPM 5.1%, [기타] 매출 
2.9조(+2%), OP 712억(-72%), OPM 2.5% 기록

2027년 매출 191조(YoY +2%, 이하 YoY), OP 12.7조(+15%), OPM 6.6% 전망 
기저효과 바탕으로 연결 도매 물량 YoY +5% 증가 예상하나, 원달러 환율 -
4% 하락(가정 환율 1,380원)하며 1H27 → 2H27로가며 이익체력 개선 예상 
(단위: 십억원, %)

### Page 5

DAISHIN SECURITIES

5  
  현대차 3Q26 실적 컨퍼런스콜 QnA 
Q. 금융부문 환율 변동에 따른 4Q26 매출 및 손익 영향 
- 금융부문 환율 영향은 현대차 실적에 미치는 환율 영향과 동일한 방향으로 발생

Q. 3Q26 금융부문 수익성 악화 요인 
- 장기금리 상승에 따른 조달 및 이자비용 증가가 주요인. 자산/부채 만기 매칭에 따른 
금리 상승 영향 반영 
- EV 중심 중고차 가격지수 소폭 하락 및 차량 반환에 따른 매각손실, 금리 상승에 따
른 연체율 및 신용비용 상승 
- 차량 판매가격 인하 영향은 없으며, 3Q26 도매 판매 감소에 따른 금융 영업수익 영향 
불가피했음

Q. 향후 주주환원 정책에서 배당 및 자사주 매입 우선순위 
- 기존 밸류업 정책: 최소 DPS 1만원/TPR 35% 제시. 연간 배당 약 2.6조원 → TPR 
35% 충족 위한 잔여분 자사주 매입/소각 활용 
- 경영환경 변화 고려하여 기존 밸류업 프로그램 전반의 주주가치 제고 방안 재검토중 
- 새로운 정책 확정 및 주주 소통 전까지 기존 약속인 최소 DPS 1만원 유지할 계획 
- 시장의 배당 및 자사주 매입 간 균형 요구를 인지하고 있으며, 주주 의견 수렴 후 주
주가치 제고 계획 공유 예정

Q. 3Q26 영업이익 증감 요인 중 기타 +6,400억 발생 원인 
- 월별 컨틴전시 활동을 통한 비용 최적화 및 재료비 절감 지속 추진 
- 4Q26 원가 절감 효과는 3Q26 대비 확대 예상

Q. 3Q26 영업외손익 감소 원인 
- 3Q26 환율 급락으로 외화 매출채권 등 외화자산 관련 외화환산손실 및 외환거래손실 
발생 
- 매출채권 인식 시점과 회수 시점 간 환율 차이에 따른 외환차손 발생 
- 지분법 대상 기업들의 3Q26 실적 부진에 따른 지분법손익 감소도 일부 영향. 대부분 
외환 관련 손실에서 발생

Q. 중국 OEM 국내 진출 확대에 따른 대응 및 신차/가격 전략 
- 중국 OEM 및 테슬라의 국내 M/S 확대 대응중. 다만, 잦은 가격 조정은 중고차 잔존
가치 및 고객가치 훼손할 수 있어 지양 
- 현대캐피탈의 캡티브 금융 경쟁력 활용. 잔존가치 유예할부 등을 통해 월 납입금을 낮
춰 중국 OEM 대비 경쟁력 확보 
- 서비스 네트워크 기반으로 경쟁사 대비 경쟁력 확보 노력 
- 국내 신차: 4Q26 아반떼 HEV, 투싼, GV90, G80 HEV, 싼타페 FL(2026.12월), G90 
FL 등 출시 예정 
- 북미: 4Q26 투싼, GV80 HEV 출시 및 27년 초 GV90 출시 예상 
- 유럽: 아이오닉3 판매 개시, 4Q26 투싼/베이온 등 주요 모델 출시 예정 
- 중국: 아이오닉 V / 인도: 베이온, 아이오닉 P 등 출시 예정 
- 신차 출시 계획 연도별로 다수 예정. 가격은 원가/경쟁사/소비자 가격 수용성 등을 고
려해 결정

Q. 3Q26 법인세비용 증가 원인 및 4Q26 전망 
- 일회성 요인보다는 법인별 손익 믹스 및 적용 세율 차이에 따른 영향 
- 4Q26 법인세 수준은 QoQ 큰 변동 없을 것으로 전망

## Document 257: [207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더할 모멘텀삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더_21961458

Source PDF: all report pdf/[207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더할 모멘텀삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더_21961458.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-07
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
미국 록빌·5공장 가세로 3분기 기대치 부합 전망 
삼성바이오로직스의 2026년 3분기 연결 매출액은 1조 3,637억 원(+8.4% Y oY), 
영업이익은 6,092억 원(-3.8% YoY, 영업이익률 44.7%)으로 시장 기대치에 부합할 
전망이다. 5월 파업에 따른 생산 차질에도 1~4공장(총 60.4만 리터)의 풀가동과 
신규 공장의 매출 기여가 외형 성장을 뒷받침할 것으로 예상한다. 미국 록빌 
공장(6만 리터)은 3분기부터 매출 기여가 본격화되고, 5공장(18만 리터)도 
인증용(PPQ) 배치 생산을 통해 매출이 확대될 전망이다. 2분기에는 미국 공장·5공장 
관련 비용이 선반영됐으나, 3분기에는 매출 증가에 따른 고정비 부담 완화로 
영업이익률이 전분기 대비 0.3%p 상승할 것으로 예상한다. 
항체 중심 CDMO에서 펩타이드로 확장 
삼성바이오로직스의 2026년 신규 수주는 약 1.2조 원으로 전년 수주액의 약 20% 
수준에 그치고 있으나, 연말부터 내년까지 수주 모멘텀은 점차 강화될 것으로 
판단한다. 2025년 하반기 이후 미국의 의약품 관세 불확실성이 완화되며 글로벌 
제약사와의 논의가 늘어나고 있어, 신규 수주 계약으로 이어질 것으로 기대된다. 
11월 유상증자 납입이 마무리되면 6공장(18만 리터) 착공과 스위스 폴리펩타이드 
인수가 본격화되며 중장기 성장 기대감도 높아질 전망이다. 특히 폴리펩타이드 
인수가 연내 완료되면 2027년부터 연결 실적에 반영되는 동시에, 항체 의약품 
중심의 CDMO 사업을 GLP-1 등 펩타이드로 확장하는 계기가 될 전망이다. 
목표주가 200만 원으로 하향, 수주 회복이 관건 
삼성바이오로직스의 목표주가를 209만 원에서 200만 원으로 하향한다. 유상증자 
신주 발행에 따른 주당가치 4.7% 희석 효과를 반영했다. 폴리펩타이드 인수 효과는 
거래완료 이후 실적 추정치에 반영할 예정이다. 노조 파업 불확실성이 주가에 
부담으로 작용할 수 있어 신규 수주 모멘텀의 회복이 필요한 시점이다. 
목표주가 (하향) 2,000,000원  
현재가 (10/6) 1,310,000원

당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

## Document 323: Chartpack _ Advanced economies_ Resilience to shock after shock (2026-10-06)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Chartpack _ Advanced economies_ Resilience to shock after shock (2026-10-06).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-06
Pages: 12
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

By Advanced Economies Team
Advanced Economies:
Resilience to shock after shock
October 2026

### Page 2

• The global economy is in the midst of a growth cycle. As a result, GDP growth is more resilient to shocks than initially
anticipated.
• The figures for the second quarter bear this out, despite the sharp rise in oil prices, whose negative impact on growth is offset
by a series of reflationary forces.
• The driving force behind the current growth cycle is clear: artificial intelligence (AI).
• The world’s major economies are currently engaged in a broader push to expand their capital goods investment.

• While European and US households are experiencing more or less the same energy price shock, inflation and household
consumption remain higher in the US.
• Higher and above-target inflation calls for monetary tightening.
• Inflation is exceeding the target set by three of the four central banks examined in this publication (the US, the UK, the
Eurozone). Inflation is currently expected to peak in Q4 2026 or Q1 2027, but there is a significant risk that energy inflation will

spread further to other components of inflation in the future.
• Inflation can also be a sign of (too much) economic strength (in the US).
• Monetary policies also have to offset the inflationary effects of fiscal policy (particularly in Japan).
• The outbreak of the conflict in Iran acted as a catalyst: the ensuing rise in energy prices contributes to the recent increase in
long-term rates (which had been trending downwards at the start of the year) to levels close to those of 2002.

• The other issue is the high level of fiscal deficits (too high for a period of growth), reflecting different fiscal trajectories. In the
US, the UK, France and Germany the public debt-to-GDP ratio is increasing, while it has stabilized in Italy and decreased in
Spain and Japan.
• The rise in long-term interest rates will weigh on public finances. The fiscal arithmetic is simpler today than it will be in 2030.
By 2030, the effective rate will have risen and caught up with GDP growth.

For more information, please refer to our publication:
EcoPerspectives – Advanced Economies | 3rd quarter of 2026, as of September 24, 2026– Economic Research– BNP Paribas
Advanced economies: Resilience to shock after shock

## Document 407: NZ総選挙は与党連合が辛うじて過半数確保も、政権の行方は流動的 ～政権の枠組み次第では金融政策の目標が見直される可能性、ＮＺ_22dd47d2

Source PDF: all report pdf/NZ総選挙は与党連合が辛うじて過半数確保も、政権の行方は流動的 ～政権の枠組み次第では金融政策の目標が見直される可能性、ＮＺ_22dd47d2.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-09
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026 年 11 月 9 日

1/5 
NZ 総選挙は与党連合が辛うじて過半数確保も、政権の行方は流動的 
～政権の枠組み次第では金融政策の目標が見直される可能性、ＮＺドル相場にも不透明感～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ ニュージーランドでは、11 月７日に議会総選挙が実施され、 暫定結果では国民党を中心とす
る与党３党が合計67 議席を獲得し、過半数をわずか１議席上回った。ただし、約40 万票の
特別票が未集計であり、11 月27 日の公式結果の公表まで政権の行方は不透明な状況が続く。
今回の選挙では、物価高による生活費負担の増大や雇用環境の悪化、景気回復の遅れなどを
背景に、既存の二大政党への支持が低下し、中小政党が存在感を強めた。 
○ ニュージーランド準備銀行 （RBNZ）は10 月の定例会合で、 市場予想に反して政策金利を2.75％
に据え置いた。インフレ率が目標レンジを上回る一方、雇用環境の悪化や景気減速への懸念
が強まっており、金融政策の判断は難しさを増している。また、総選挙後に労働党を中心と
する政権が成立した場合には、金融政策の目標に雇用の最大化を再び加える「デュアル・マ
ンデート」が復活する可能性がある。ただし、政策目標の変更が直ちに金融緩和につながる
とは限らず、中銀の独立性を維持した政策判断が重要となる。 
○ 先行きは、12 月９日の次回会合で追加利上げが検討される可能性があるものの、 その判断は
インフレ圧力の持続性や景気・雇用動向に左右されよう。ＮＺドル相場は、RBNZ の追加利上
げ観測が下支え要因となる一方、政権の枠組みを巡る不透明感や豪州との金利差拡大などが
重石となり、当面は上値の重い展開が続くと見込まれる。さらに、景気の下振れや世界的な
リスク回避姿勢の強まりによっては、 ＮＺドル安が一段と進む可能性にも注意が必要である。

【総選挙は与党連合が過半数を暫定的に確保も、政権の行方は依然として流動的】 
ニュージーランドでは、11 月７日に議会（代議院：一院制）の総選挙が実施された。同国では、
小選挙区比例代表併用制（ＭＭＰ）が採用されており、原則として政党票の得票率に応じて議席が
配分される一方、 単独政党が過半数を獲得することは難しく、 複数政党による連立政権が形成され
てきた。 今回の総選挙を巡っては、2023 年に発足したラクソン首相率いる国民党、ＡＣＴ党、ＮＺ
ファースト党の中道右派連立政権が継続するか、 ヒプキンス党首率いる最大野党・労働党を中心と
する政権への交代が実現するかが焦点となった。

### Page 2

2/5 
選挙管理委員会が公表した暫定集計によれば、政党票の得票率は労働党が 29.36％、国民党が
29.21％と、労働党がわずかに上回った。
一方で、暫定的な議席配分では、国民党
が 42 議席、労働党が36 議席となったほ
か、緑の党が16 議席、ＡＣＴ党が13 議
席、ＮＺファースト党が12 議席、 新興の
オポチュニティ党が７議席、マオリ党が
５議席となった（図１）。 国民党、ＡＣＴ
党、ＮＺファースト党の与党３党は合計
67 議席を獲得する計算となり、 暫定的な
総議席数131 議席における過半数 （66 議
席）をわずか１議席上回った。ただし、
現時点では在外投票をはじめとする特別票 （約 40 万票）が未集計であり、11 月27 日の公式結果の
発表まで、最終的な議席配分は確定しない。このため、与党連合による政権維持が確実になった訳
ではなく、各党間の連立協議や新興政党の対応も含め、政治情勢の不透明感が続くであろう。 
今回の選挙では、国民党が政党票の得票率に基づく配分議席を上回る 42 小選挙区を獲得したた
め、７つの超過議席が発生した。マオリ党についても４つの超過議席が生じ、議会の総議席数は定
数の 120 議席から 131 議席に大幅に拡大する見通しとなっている。こうした選挙制度上の特徴が、
国民党の政党票が労働党を下回ったにもかかわらず、 与党連合が議席数で過半数を確保する要因と
なっている。一方、今回初めて議席獲得に必要な得票率５％の壁を超えたオポチュニティ党は、最
終結果次第で連立協議の行方を左右する存在となる可能性がある。また、2023 年の前回総選挙で
は、 特別票の集計により国民党が暫定結果から２議席を失うなど議席配分に変化が生じており、 今
回も公式結果の公表を待つ必要がある。

【物価高と雇用環境の悪化が有権者の不満を強め、二大政党離れが進む結果に】 
今回の総選挙では、有権者の投票行動
を左右した重要な要因として、物価高に
よる生活費負担の増大と景気回復の遅れ
が挙げられる。同国経済は、2024 年から
2025 年にかけて、 高金利や内需低迷を背
景に厳しい状況に直面した。2025 年末か
ら 2026 年初めにかけて景気に持ち直し
の動きが確認されたものの、家計消費や
住宅投資の回復は鈍く、輸出関連産業と
内需関連産業の間で景況感の乖離が続い
ている。 加えて、2026 年に入って以降は、

3/5 
イラン情勢の悪化による原油価格の上昇が家計の実質購買力を圧迫する要因となった。 こうしたな
か、７-９月のインフレ率は前年比＋3.7％と、前期（同＋4.1％）から鈍化したものの、RBNZ の目
標レンジ（１～３％）を上回っている（図２）。さらに、７-９月の失業率は5.7％と約 11 年ぶりの
高水準となるなど、家計を取り巻く環境は厳しさを増している。 
こうした経済環境は、 有権者の政治に対する評価に影響を与えたと考えられる。 今回の暫定結果
では、国民党と労働党の得票率の合計が６割を下回った一方、緑の党やＡＣＴ党、ＮＺファースト
党、オポチュニティ党などが支持を広げた。これは、既存政党の経済運営に対する不満に加え、有
権者の政策志向が多様化していることを示唆している。とはいえ、 各党の支持拡大の背景は一様で
はなく、経済・財政政策の違いに加えて、環境問題、移民政策、先住民マオリの権利なども投票判
断に影響したとみられる。また、選挙管理委員会の推計による投票率は 74.6％と、前回の 78.2％
を下回った。今回の結果は、与党に対する評価だけでなく、野党を含む既存の二大政党に対する有
権者の評価が分かれたことを反映したものと捉えられる。

【RBNZは10 月会合で予想外の金利据え置き、インフレと景気減速リスクの間で難しい判断に直面】 
RBNZ による金融政策も総選挙の重要な争点のひとつとなった。RBNZ は、2026 年７月と９月の定
例会合において、それぞれ 25bp の利上げを決定し、政策金利であるオフィシャルキャッシュレー
ト（ＯＣＲ）を2.75％まで引き上げてき
た。しかし、10 月の定例会合では、事前
には幅広く 25bp の追加利上げが予想さ
れていたにもかかわらず、政策金利を据
え置く決定を行った（注１） （図３）。こ
の決定を巡っては、７人の金融政策委員
のうち、ブレマン総裁ら４人が据え置き
を支持する一方、 残る３人が25bp の利上
げを主張する僅差の採決となった。これ
は、RBNZ の内部においても、景気への影
響を見極めるかを巡って判断が分かれて
いることを示唆している。 
RBNZ が追加利上げを見送った背景には、 物価上昇圧力が続く一方、 その持続性について慎重に判
断する必要があるとの認識がある。10 月の定例会合後に発表された声明文では、７-９月のインフ
レ率が事前予想を下回り、 賃金上昇率も抑制されており、 中期的なインフレ期待も安定しているこ
とを指摘した。 さらに、 ７月と９月に実施した利上げの影響を見極める必要があるとの見方が示さ
れた。一方、基調的なインフレ率は目標レンジの上限付近まで高まり、価格上昇が幅広い品目に広

がっていることに警戒感を示した。 加えて、イラン情勢の悪化による原油価格の高止まりやＮＺド
ル安が輸入物価を押し上げることから、10-12 月のインフレ率は前年同期比＋4.4％に再加速する
と予想している。 このため、RBNZ は実際の政策判断については今後の経済・物価動向を見極める姿
勢を示した。

【総選挙後の政権の枠組み次第では金融政策の目標が見直される可能性も、RBNZの独立性が重要に】 
総選挙後の金融政策を考えるうえで重要なのが、RBNZ の政策目標を巡る与野党間の考え方の違
いであろう。 国民党を中心とする現政権は、2023 年に金融政策の目標から最大限持続可能な雇用の
達成を削除し、物価安定に重点を置く政策枠組みへと変更した。一方で、労働党は今回の総選挙の
公約に、物価安定と最大限持続可能な雇用の達成を併せて重視する「デュアル・マンデート」の復
活を掲げた。このため、 新政権の構成次第では、RBNZ の政策運営を巡る制度的な枠組みが再び見直
される可能性がある。 
とはいえ、 政策目標の見直しが直ちに利下げや金融緩和につながるとは限らない。 仮に労働党を
中心とする政権が成立し、 最大限持続可能な雇用の達成 という目標が復活した場合 においても、
RBNZ には物価安定を維持する責務が引き続き課される。 インフレ率が目標レンジを上回るなか、雇
用環境の悪化を考慮しながらも、物価上昇圧力の持続性を慎重に見極める必要がある。また、政権
交代に伴う財政政策の変更が需要やインフレ期待に与える影響によっては、 政策金利の引き下げ余
地が制約される可能性もある。一方、国民党を中心とする連立政権が継続する場合、財政規律の維
持によるインフレ抑制効果が期待されるものの、 歳出抑制が家計消費や雇用の回復を鈍らせる可能
性もある。いずれの政権が成立しても、金融政策は政府の意向ではなく、中銀が経済・物価見通し
に基づいて独立して判断するという基本原則が重要となる。 
10 月会合での金利据え置き決定を巡っては、 総選挙直前のタイミングであったため、 その政治的
な影響にも関心が集まった。 ブレマン総裁は定例会合後の記者会見において、 総選挙の日程は金融
政策の判断に影響していないと説明し、 政策目標の達成に必要な経済情勢の評価を優先していると
強調した。このため、金利据え置きの決定を選挙対策や政治的配慮によるものとみなすことは適切
ではない。 金融政策委員会の見解が僅差で分かれたことそのものが、 インフレ抑制と景気への配慮
の間で政策判断が難しくなっていることを示唆している。

【RBNZは12 月会合で追加利上げを再検討か、NZドル相場は政治情勢と金利見通しに左右される】 
先行きの金融政策については、12 月９日に予定される次回会合が重要な節目となる。RBNZ は 10
月会合において、 今後公表される経済指標が政策判断を左右する可能性を指摘しており、 追加利上
げの選択肢を維持している。金融市場の見通しも、12 月会合での 25bp の利上げを見込む向きがあ
り、2027 年にかけて政策金利が 3.25～4.00％程度まで引き上げられるとの予想が示されている。
とはいえ、足元のインフレ圧力が一時的な供給要因にとどまる場合には追加利上げを急ぐ必要性が
低下する一方で、 輸入物価の上昇が賃金やサービス価格に波及する場合には、 さらなる金融引き締
めが必要となる可能性がある。

### Page 5

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
5/5 
金融市場においては、ＮＺドル相場に下押し圧力がかかる動きがみられる。また、10 月会合での
予想外の金利据え置きを受けて、 ＮＺド
ルは対米ドルで急落した（図４）。先行き
のＮＺドル相場については、RBNZ の追加
利上げ観測が下支え要因となる一方、 政
権の枠組みを巡る不透明感が上値を抑
える可能性がある。 国民党を中心とする
与党連合が最終的に過半数を確保でき
ない場合には、 連立協議の長期化や政策
運営の不透明感が意識されやすくなる。
さらに、 オーストラリア準備銀行 （ＲＢ
Ａ）が 11 月会合で政策金利を4.85％に
引き上げたことで、両国の政策金利差が拡大していることも、 対豪ドルでＮＺドルの上値を抑える
要因となり得る（注２）。 
短期的にはＮＺドル相場の上値が重い展開が続くと見込まれる。先行きについては、 インフレ圧
力が持続して RBNZ による利上げ観測が強まれば、ＮＺドルが下支えされる可能性はある。政治情
勢の安定化によって不確実性が低下した場合には、これまでの調整の反動も重なり、 ＮＺドル相場
の持ち直しにつながることも考えられる。一方で、雇用環境が一段と悪化するとともに、景気が下
振れして追加利上げ観測が後退すれば、ＮＺドル安が一段と進む可能性もある。加えて、イラン情
勢の悪化に伴う原油高など、世界的なリスク回避姿勢が強まる場合には、 国内金利の上昇にもかか
わらずＮＺドル安が進む可能性がある点には注意が必要である。

### Page 3

Report Introduction
Our proprietary dbDataInsights survey shows cyber threats are evolving faster than organisations can adapt
Source: dbDataInsights survey of cybersecurity experts, September 2026; Cybersecurity Survey 2026, Deutsche Bank Research.
“It’s the only thing that can shut down my business.”
That was the punchline from a conversation I had recently with the CEO of a large
privately held financial services company. This was his comment when I followed up

as to why cybersecurity risk is the number one concern that “keeps him up at night”.
To help understand this risk, our dbDataInsights team surveyed more than 50
cybersecurity experts. The uncomfortable conclusion is that many firms are
unprepared for cyber threats. Cyber risk is increasing as AI adoption accelerates.
—89% agree the threat landscape is evolving faster than organisations can adapt.
—78% say the gap between highly and poorly prepared organisations is widening.

What has changed with AI advancement? Cyberattacks have become faster,
cheaper, more deceptive and more scalable. Capabilities once reserved for
sophisticated actors are now broadly accessible to a new generation of profit-driven
criminals. At the same time, rising geopolitical tension has made cyber operations
an increasingly attractive low-cost, deniable tool for nation states. Corporates and
investors therefore need to understand their broader exposure and critical

infrastructure risk in their supply chain and with stakeholders.
Among other autonomous incidents, the recent Hugging Face incident
highlighted AI’s cyberattack potential. AI agents caused harm without
direction from a human attacker, with weak guardrails and no human
judgement to restrain them. Beyond the systemic questions it raised, the attack
highlights three additional concerns: (i) AI operates at a speed and scale humans
cannot match, with agents taking over 17,000+ actions in four days; (ii) the intrusion

began in the data-processing pipeline, where AI platforms are uniquely exposed;
and (iii) the agents escaped their test environment through a widely used third-party
tool representing supplier risk in an unfamiliar form.

### Page 4

Report summary and notable takeaways
Secular trends and AI adoption have widened the attack surface; the severity of losses is compounding
Source: FBI Internet Crime Complaint Center (IC3), company security advisories, OECD, company reports, Deutsche Bank Research.
Secular trends have widened the attack surface. This includes rapid
digitalisation, widespread Internet of Things (IoT) endpoints, hybrid workforces,
cloud adoption and deeper supply chain dependencies. The scale of the threat

increase is reflected in data from the FBI Internet Crime Complaint Center (IC3),
which shows cybercrime complaints more than doubled from 2019 pre-pandemic
baseline of ~467,000 to ~1.0mn in 2025, with a 3x expansion relative to 2017 levels.
Barriers to entry are lower; attack tools are now sold “off the shelf”. Today,
cybercrime marketplaces, available on the dark web, look less like the underworld
portrayed in films and more like an online software store. Buyers can access AI-

powered tools that scan for software vulnerabilities (see top-right chart showing
reported CVEs after the release of cyber capable frontier models), write malicious
code at speed, and provide access to malware that rewrites its own code to avoid
detection as it runs. Ransomware-as-a-service (RaaS) takes it further, renting out
ready-made attack kits, often in exchange for a portion of the profits.
Impersonation is becoming harder to detect. Hackers can now generate more

realistic phishing messages, while voice cloning and deepfakes make impersonation
convincing enough to gain access unnoticed. With stolen credentials, attackers often
aren’t hacking in, they are simply logging in.
The rise of loose cybercriminal collectives. Some of the most damaging recent
attacks have been linked to loosely organised, often English-speaking networks such
as ShinyHunters and Scattered Spider. The latter has been widely named as the

group behind the 2025 Marks & Spencer cyberattack, in which the hacking group
deployed RaaS. AI is enabling these groups to become larger and faster, widening
the gap between the speed of attack and a defender’s response.
The severity of losses is compounding. A 2026 IBM study puts the average breach
at a record USD ~5.0mn, up 12% YoY, but the impact on large companies can run
into hundreds of millions or even billions. Estimates for global cyber insurance

premiums (per Munich Re) point to a rise from roughly USD 7.0bn in 2020 to USD
15.0bn in 2025, with expectations to reach USD 28.0bn by 2030.

### Page 5

5
Among the more concerning survey takeaways is that a large-scale and
significant systemic incident is now the base case.
— 83% expect a significant systemic attack within two years and 81% expect it to be
moderately, very, or extremely severe for economic activity and markets.
— 81% say AI favours attackers today, with not one respondent putting defenders
ahead of attackers. Only 20% expect defenders to be ahead in five years.
Geopolitical tensions coupled with rising cyber capabilities have transformed

cyberspace into a digital battlefield between global powers. Intent has moved
towards espionage and disruption of not only critical infrastructure (e.g., healthcare,
finance, power, industrial systems, defence) but private companies as well. Chinese
President Xi Jinping has linked cybersecurity to national security and economic and
social stability, with China’s public statements similarly emphasising the protection
of critical infrastructure. At the same time, the US’s June 2025 Executive Order on

Cybersecurity reflects its continued focus on national cyber resilience.
Europe relies on US cyber tools while its start-ups lag in funding. Reliance on US
security vendors exposes European firms to potential access, update or export
restrictions over the long-term. Shrinking this gap will prove difficult given US cyber
start-ups attract nearly 6x the venture and growth funding of European peers. And
while the timing of quantum computing (which could break widely used encryption

methods) remains uncertain, it could further deepen Europe’s dependency.
Ultimately, cyber preparedness is a financial variable. Investors should ask what
firms spend on security versus AI adoption, how well they know their vulnerabilities,
and how fast they can recover.
— Only 24% of respondents think organisations have sufficient visibility into the
threats relevant to them, even though 57% saw cybersecurity budgets rise over
the past 12 months.

— 81% agree that cybersecurity related laws and regulation cannot keep pace, and
65% doubt leadership understands cyber risk well enough.

think a systemic cyber incident is likely
within two years

### Page 6

6
When cyber weakness = share price consequence…
Investors are more aware of cyber risk today, but they rarely sell a stock due to
perceived cyber vulnerabilities (although we are aware of some who have).
—What is the potential impact? Our dbDataInsights event study of 47 breaches
shows a cumulative breach-attributed return of c. -2.9% within the first two
trading days and c. -6.0% through first 20 days trading days. The erosion then
continues more gradually, reaching c. -6.7% nine months after disclosure (see

slide 13).
Which industries are most vulnerable?
—Our survey respondents point to (1) government & public sector (52%), (2)
financial services (50%), (3) energy and utilities (46%) and (4) healthcare and life
sciences follow (39%, rising to 48% among US respondents).
We should caveat, that high sector exposure does not mean every company in it is
equally vulnerable. Some firms in these “exposed” sectors may very well be better
prepared given they have built specialist teams and defence protocols for decades.

What about access to credit and ratings? Credit and rating providers are paying
attention. Moody’s and S&P both cut their outlook on Jaguar Land Rover within two
months of its 2025 hack. Today, this type of impact is not widespread. But if risk
escalates through a systemic incident or an AI-driven step change in attacks these
mechanisms, including ESG ratings, could reprice asset ownership or impact access
to credit. In fact, some lenders are already pricing it. Journal of Corporate Finance

research using US syndicated loan data found borrowers with persistently higher cyber
risk pay 4–13bp more.
The bottom line? While a broader systemic attack is hard to prepare for, corporates
and investors should be asking questions about vulnerabilities, security investment,
preparedness and recovery should an attack occur. This is especially true for
industries quickly adopting AI capabilities and / or a high threat landscape.

dbDataInsights Cybersecurity Expert Survey methodology
—This survey was conducted among 54 cybersecurity professionals across the US
and Europe in September of 2026. Questions took the form of either multiple

choice selections or free form responses. The dbDataInsights team received the
responses anonymously, but respondents were required to work or consult in
cyber security roles
—Who took the survey? On self-identified roles, around 80% sit inside an
organisation’s cybersecurity function, with the remainder working independently
(15%) or in academia and research (6%). The most common titles were security
architect (23%), Chief Information Security Officer (16%) and security analyst (9%),

alongside Head of Security, Deputy CISO, Incident Response Lead and Security
Operations Lead.
—Only a small minority of respondents work for cyber resellers, thus survey views
are primarily attributable to those working within an organization on
cybersecurity.
—Survey participants were also asked for their perspectives on cyber resilience and
governance, what is misunderstood, alongside emerging threats, risk to AI
adoption and the influence of geopolitics.

—While responses were anonymous, in some cases we conducted follow up
interviews with certain participants where contact details were made available.
Source: dbDataInsights survey of cybersecurity experts, September 2026; Cybersecurity Survey 2026, Deutsche Bank Research.

### Page 10

Risk from AI-enabled attacks and added AI adoption vulnerabilities
Insider threats and supply chain compromises remain a growing concern
“The fear of AI writing undetectable malware is … overblown. Attackers were
never short of malware, since they can buy it ready made along with initial
access. Good detection looks at behaviour rather than exact code, so a rewritten
payload still has to do the same suspicious things.”
“Frontier AI models are currently the main hype. Automated phishing and

scams are underappreciated, yet they are responsible for the bigger
monetary losses at the moment.”
“There are too many organisations relying on the same providers for
critical services and this means single points of failure.”
“A corporation might be very secure but if they rely on a third party that
does not have the same level of security posture, [it] could be
compromised via this third-party (basically their weak link).”
“Existing employee login behaviour and usage of AI is underappreciated –

it’s what poses the biggest threat to a breach.”
“No company can guarantee they won’t be breached, so there needs to
be more focus put on the ability to recover and survive an attack.”
Source: dbDataInsights survey of cybersecurity experts, September 2026; Cybersecurity Survey 2026, Deutsche Bank Research .

### Page 11

11
Experts believe governments are not keeping pace, and no one owns AI oversight
How experts surveyed by dbDataInsights view governance and regulation
—AI adoption is outpacing internal controls. As one expert put it: “Companies
are pushing the use of AI more to their employees without the proper
mechanisms and policies in place to prevent misuse or attacks initiated by the AI
systems themselves.”
—Regulation is struggling to keep pace. 81% of respondents agree that

cybersecurity-related laws and regulations are failing to keep up with
technological change, and 54% say regulatory requirements can improve security
but may also slow innovation. One respondent highlighted “the levels of
uncertainty, the long running nature of the problems, and the rapidity of change.”
—Responsibility for AI oversight remains unsettled. International bodies are the
leading choice, supported by 50% of respondents. No option commands a

majority, underscoring the lack of consensus on who should oversee AI
development.
—Better board questions may not guarantee better decisions. The survey
points to more informed questioning by corporate leadership, yet 65% doubt that
leadership teams understand cyber risk well enough to make informed business
trade-offs. Measuring activity more effectively than outcomes further complicates
oversight.
—Accountability is becoming personal. Only 43% say accountability for cyber risk

is clearly defined in their governance framework, yet the consequences now reach
the top as a handful of CEOs have left or gone into early retirement after
financially consequential cyber breaches.
Who should primarily oversee AI development?

Material cyber breaches can cause a notable drop in share price
Our dbDataInsights study found a 2.9% drop on average within two trading days, with the cumulative breach-attributed return of -6.0% on trading day 20.

Source: dbDataInsights, Bloomberg Finance LP., Deutsche Bank Research, Company filings. Event study of reported cybersecurity incidents, 2015 -2025, 34 US and 13 European companies across nine sectors.
What typically happens to a company’s shares when a breach is reported?
—Short-term impact: The market prices the breach immediately. Our model shows
cumulative breach-attributed (i.e. isolating only the part of the share price move
we can pin on the breach itself) returns are c. -2.9% within two trading days of

at one year, although the one-year point estimate (our single best-guess figure)
should be treated with caution given the width of the confidence band.
—Pre-breach price behaviour: We find no evidence of information leakage ahead
of disclosure. While the breach component for the [-10,-1] window (ten trading
days before the news broke) is +0.04%, the corresponding t-stat value of 0.03 falls
notably below the usual bar for significance (c.2). As such, we conclude the market

had not begun reacting before the announcement.
Event study methodology
—Sample: 47 publicly reported cybersecurity incidents across the US and Europe
over 2015-2025, measured against each company’s regional sector benchmark.
Requirement of 5 years of stock returns pre-attack and 1 year of post-attack.
—Day 0: the day an incident was first publicly reported or disclosed.
—Baseline: We use five years of pre-incident share-price performance to
estimate: (i) Beta (β): how sensitive a company’s returns are to movements in its

sector and (ii) Alpha (α): the extent to which the company typically outperforms
or underperforms its sector after adjusting for that sensitivity.
—Attribution: post-incident returns are split into sector-relative returns, alpha
(α) and breach (abnormal returns attributed to the disclosure).
—95% confidence interval: Range within which we can say with 95% confidence
that the real share price impact lies, based on our sample data.
Total return attribution

95% Confidence Interval Breach impact negative at 95% confidence Cumulative impact of cyberincident
